  359: 
  360: function findOption(options, value) {
  361:   return options.find((option) => option.value === value) || null;
  362: }
  363: 
  364: export function getSkillCategoryOption(value) {
  365:   return findOption(SKILL_CATEGORY_OPTIONS, value);
  366: }
  367:

FILE: frontend\src\models\skillModel.js

Total lines: 452
SHA256: 5C127C85958BE2170788A37DD481DB77FEF30DE6EEBE233FFDB7C81659AD13FC
    1: import {
    2:   DEFAULT_SKILL_VALUES,
    3:   isValidLanguageProficiency,
    4:   isValidSkillCategory,
    5:   isValidSkillProficiency,
    6:   isValidSkillSource,
    7:   isValidSkillStatus,
    8:   isValidSkillType,
    9:   SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS,
   10: } from "../config/skillConfig.js";
   11: 
   12: /*
   13:  * =========================================
   14:  * Model Version
   15:  * =========================================
   16:  */
   17: 
   18: export const SKILL_MODEL_VERSION = 1;
   19: 
   20: /*
   21:  * =========================================
   22:  * ID Creation
   23:  * =========================================
   24:  */
   25: 
   26: export function createSkillId(prefix = "skill") {
   27:   if (
   28:     typeof crypto !== "undefined" &&
   29:     typeof crypto.randomUUID === "function"
   30:   ) {
   31:     return `${prefix}-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
   32:   }
   33: 
   34:   return `${prefix}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`;
   35: }
   36: 
   37: /*
   38:  * =========================================
   39:  * Primitive Normalization
   40:  * =========================================
   41:  */
   42: 
   43: function normalizeText(value, maximumLength = Infinity) {
   44:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
   45:     return "";
   46:   }
   47: 
   48:   return value.trim().slice(0, maximumLength);
   49: }
   50: 
   51: function normalizeNullableNumber(value) {
   52:   if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined) {
   53:     return null;
   54:   }
   55: 
   56:   const numberValue = Number(value);
   57: 
   58:   if (!Number.isFinite(numberValue) || numberValue < 0) {
   59:     return null;
   60:   }
   61: 
   62:   return Number(numberValue.toFixed(1));
   63: }
   64: 
   65: function normalizeYearMonth(value) {
   66:   const text = normalizeText(value, 10);
   67: 
   68:   if (!text) {
   69:     return "";
   70:   }
   71: 
   72:   if (!/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/.test(text)) {
   73:     return "";
   74:   }
   75: 
   76:   return text;
   77: }
   78: 
   79: /*
   80:  * =========================================
   81:  * Search Name Normalization
   82:  * =========================================
   83:  *
   84:  * normalizedName is used for searching and
   85:  * duplicate prevention. It is not intended as
   86:  * the user-facing display name.
   87:  */
   88: 
   89: export function normalizeSkillNameForComparison(value) {
   90:   return normalizeText(value)
   91:     .normalize("NFKC")
   92:     .toLocaleLowerCase()
   93:     .replace(/[._/\\-]+/g, " ")
   94:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   95:     .trim();
   96: }
   97: 
   98: /*
   99:  * =========================================
  100:  * Alias Normalization
  101:  * =========================================
  102:  */
  103: 
  104: function normalizeAliases(aliases, skillName = "") {
  105:   if (!Array.isArray(aliases)) {
  106:     return [];
  107:   }
  108: 
  109:   const normalizedSkillName = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(skillName);
  110: 
  111:   const normalizedAliases = aliases
  112:     .map((alias) => {
  113:       if (typeof alias === "string") {
  114:         return normalizeText(alias, SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.alias);
  115:       }
  116: 
  117:       return normalizeText(
  118:         alias?.name || alias?.value || alias?.label,
  119:         SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.alias,
  120:       );
  121:     })
  122:     .filter(Boolean);
  123: 
  124:   return normalizedAliases
  125:     .filter((alias, index, collection) => {
  126:       const comparisonName = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(alias);
  127: 
  128:       if (comparisonName === normalizedSkillName) {
  129:         return false;
  130:       }
  131: 
  132:       return (
  133:         collection.findIndex(
  134:           (candidate) =>
  135:             normalizeSkillNameForComparison(candidate) === comparisonName,
  136:         ) === index
  137:       );
  138:     })
  139:     .slice(0, SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumAliases);
  140: }
  141: 
  142: /*
  143:  * =========================================
  144:  * Empty Skill
  145:  * =========================================
  146:  */
  147: 
  148: export function createEmptySkill() {
  149:   const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
  150: 
  151:   return {
  152:     modelVersion: SKILL_MODEL_VERSION,
  153: 
  154:     id: createSkillId(),
  155: 
  156:     name: "",
  157: 
  158:     normalizedName: "",
  159: 
  160:     category: DEFAULT_SKILL_VALUES.category,
  161: 
  162:     type: DEFAULT_SKILL_VALUES.type,
  163: 
  164:     description: "",
  165: 
  166:     aliases: [],
  167: 
  168:     proficiency: {
  169:       level: DEFAULT_SKILL_VALUES.proficiency,
  170: 
  171:       yearsOfExperience: null,
  172: 
  173:       lastUsedDate: "",
  174:     },
  175: 
  176:     language: {
  177:       proficiency: DEFAULT_SKILL_VALUES.languageProficiency,
  178:     },
  179: 
  180:     source: DEFAULT_SKILL_VALUES.source,
  181: 
  182:     /*
  183:      * sourceContext describes where a skill was
  184:      * created. Examples:
  185:      *
  186:      * skill-page
  187:      * experience
  188:      * resume
  189:      * portfolio
  190:      * ai-experience-suggestion
  191:      */
  192: 
  193:     sourceContext: "",
  194: 
  195:     notes: "",
  196: 
  197:     status: DEFAULT_SKILL_VALUES.status,
  198: 
  199:     createdAt: timestamp,
  200:     updatedAt: timestamp,
  201:   };
  202: }
  203: 
  204: /*
  205:  * =========================================
  206:  * Normalize Skill
  207:  * =========================================
  208:  */
  209: 
  210: export function normalizeSkill(value = {}) {
  211:   const defaults = createEmptySkill();
  212: 
  213:   /*
  214:    * Permit a simple string during migration.
  215:    */
  216: 
  217:   const source =
  218:     typeof value === "string"
  219:       ? {
  220:           name: value,
  221:         }
  222:       : value && typeof value === "object"
  223:         ? value
  224:         : {};
  225: 
  226:   const proficiency =
  227:     source.proficiency && typeof source.proficiency === "object"
  228:       ? source.proficiency
  229:       : {};
  230: 
  231:   const language =
  232:     source.language && typeof source.language === "object"
  233:       ? source.language
  234:       : {};
  235: 
  236:   const name = normalizeText(
  237:     source.name || source.label || source.value,
  238:     SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.name,
  239:   );
  240: 
  241:   const category = isValidSkillCategory(source.category)
  242:     ? source.category
  243:     : defaults.category;
  244: 
  245:   const type = isValidSkillType(source.type) ? source.type : defaults.type;
  246: 
  247:   const proficiencyLevel = isValidSkillProficiency(proficiency.level)
  248:     ? proficiency.level
  249:     : defaults.proficiency.level;
  250: 
  251:   const languageProficiency = isValidLanguageProficiency(language.proficiency)
  252:     ? language.proficiency
  253:     : defaults.language.proficiency;
  254: 
  255:   const skillSource = isValidSkillSource(source.source)
  256:     ? source.source
  257:     : defaults.source;
  258: 
  259:   const status = isValidSkillStatus(source.status)
  260:     ? source.status
  261:     : defaults.status;
  262: 
  263:   return {
  264:     modelVersion: SKILL_MODEL_VERSION,
  265: 
  266:     id: normalizeText(source.id, 200) || defaults.id,
  267: 
  268:     name,
  269: 
  270:     normalizedName: normalizeSkillNameForComparison(name),
  271: 
  272:     category,
  273: 
  274:     type,
  275: 
  276:     description: normalizeText(
  277:       source.description,
  278:       SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.description,
  279:     ),
  280: 
  281:     aliases: normalizeAliases(source.aliases, name),
  282: 
  283:     proficiency: {
  284:       level: proficiencyLevel,
  285: 
  286:       yearsOfExperience: normalizeNullableNumber(
  287:         proficiency.yearsOfExperience ?? source.yearsOfExperience,
  288:       ),
  289: 
  290:       lastUsedDate: normalizeYearMonth(
  291:         proficiency.lastUsedDate || source.lastUsedDate,
  292:       ),
  293:     },
  294: 
  295:     language: {
  296:       proficiency: languageProficiency,
  297:     },
  298: 
  299:     source: skillSource,
  300: 
  301:     sourceContext: normalizeText(source.sourceContext, 100),
  302: 
  303:     notes: normalizeText(source.notes, SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.notes),
  304: 
  305:     status,
  306: 
  307:     createdAt: normalizeText(source.createdAt, 100) || defaults.createdAt,
  308: 
  309:     updatedAt: normalizeText(source.updatedAt, 100) || defaults.updatedAt,
  310:   };
  311: }
  312: 
  313: /*
  314:  * =========================================
  315:  * Create Skill
  316:  * =========================================
  317:  */
  318: 
  319: export function createSkill(values = {}) {
  320:   const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
  321: 
  322:   return normalizeSkill({
  323:     ...values,
  324: 
  325:     id: normalizeText(values?.id, 200) || createSkillId(),
  326: 
  327:     createdAt: normalizeText(values?.createdAt, 100) || timestamp,
  328: 
  329:     updatedAt: timestamp,
  330:   });
  331: }
  332: 
  333: /*
  334:  * =========================================
  335:  * Update Skill
  336:  * =========================================
  337:  */
  338: 
  339: export function updateSkillModel(currentSkill, updates = {}) {
  340:   return normalizeSkill({
  341:     ...currentSkill,
  342:     ...updates,
  343: 
  344:     proficiency: {
  345:       ...currentSkill?.proficiency,
  346:       ...updates?.proficiency,
  347:     },
  348: 
  349:     language: {
  350:       ...currentSkill?.language,
  351:       ...updates?.language,
  352:     },
  353: 
  354:     id: currentSkill?.id,
  355: 
  356:     createdAt: currentSkill?.createdAt,
  357: 
  358:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  359:   });
  360: }
  361: 
  362: /*
  363:  * =========================================
  364:  * Normalize Skill Collection
  365:  * =========================================
  366:  */
  367: 
  368: export function normalizeSkillCollection(values) {
  369:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
  370:     return [];
  371:   }
  372: 
  373:   const normalizedSkills = values.map(normalizeSkill);
  374: 
  375:   /*
  376:    * Remove duplicate IDs and duplicate skill names.
  377:    */
  378: 
  379:   return normalizedSkills.filter((skill, index, collection) => {
  380:     const duplicateIdIndex = collection.findIndex(
  381:       (candidate) => candidate.id === skill.id,
  382:     );
  383: 
  384:     const duplicateNameIndex = collection.findIndex(
  385:       (candidate) =>
  386:         candidate.normalizedName &&
  387:         candidate.normalizedName === skill.normalizedName,
  388:     );
  389: 
  390:     return (
  391:       duplicateIdIndex === index &&
  392:       (!skill.normalizedName || duplicateNameIndex === index)
  393:     );
  394:   });
  395: }
  396: 
  397: /*
  398:  * =========================================
  399:  * Find Matching Skill
  400:  * =========================================
  401:  */
  402: 
  403: export function findMatchingSkill(skills, name) {
  404:   if (!Array.isArray(skills)) {
  405:     return null;
  406:   }
  407: 
  408:   const normalizedName = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(name);
  409: 
  410:   if (!normalizedName) {
  411:     return null;
  412:   }
  413: 
  414:   return (
  415:     skills.find((skill) => {
  416:       if (normalizeSkillNameForComparison(skill.name) === normalizedName) {
  417:         return true;
  418:       }
  419: 
  420:       return (skill.aliases || []).some(
  421:         (alias) => normalizeSkillNameForComparison(alias) === normalizedName,
  422:       );
  423:     }) || null
  424:   );
  425: }
  426: 
  427: /*
  428:  * =========================================
  429:  * Skill Display Information
  430:  * =========================================
  431:  */
  432: 
  433: export function getSkillDisplayName(skill) {
  434:   return normalizeText(skill?.name) || "Unnamed Skill";
  435: }
  436: 
  437: /*
  438:  * =========================================
  439:  * Public Skill
  440:  * =========================================
  441:  *
  442:  * Private notes and internal source context are
  443:  * excluded before public presentation.
  444:  */
  445: 
  446: export function createPublicSkill(value) {
  447:   const skill = normalizeSkill(value);
  448: 
  449:   const { notes, sourceContext, ...publicSkill } = skill;
  450: 
  451:   return publicSkill;
  452: }

   35: function normalizeSkillContextError(error) {
   36:   return {
   37:     message:
   38:       error?.publicMessage ||
   39:       error?.message ||
   40:       "An unexpected Skill Library error occurred.",
   41: 
   42:     code: error?.code || "SKILL_OPERATION_FAILED",
   43: 
   44:     status: Number(error?.status) || 500,

   49:

   51:

   53:       error?.fieldErrors && typeof error.fieldErrors === "object"
   54:         ? error.fieldErrors
   55:         : {},

   70: function createSkillDeletionConflict(skillId, usage) {
   71:   const experienceCount = Number(usage?.experienceCount) || 0;
   72: 
   73:   const error = new Error(`Skill "${skillId}" is still referenced.`);
   74: 
   75:   error.name = "SkillUsageConflictError";
   76:   error.code = "SKILL_IN_USE";
   77:   error.status = 409;
   78: 
   79:   error.publicMessage =
   80:     experienceCount === 1
   81:       ? "This skill cannot be permanently deleted because it is used by an experience. Archive it instead."
   82:       : `This skill cannot be permanently deleted because it is used by ${experienceCount} experiences. Archive it instead.`;
   83: 
   84:   error.usage = usage;
   85: 
   86:   error.conflicts = [
   87:     {
   88:       field: "skillId",
   89:       code: "skill_in_use",
   90:       message: error.publicMessage,
   91:       skillId,
   92:       experienceCount,
   93: 
   94:       experienceSkillCount: Number(usage?.experienceSkillCount) || 0,
   95: 
   96:       experienceTechnologyCount: Number(usage?.experienceTechnologyCount) || 0,
   97: 
   98:       experienceIds: Array.isArray(usage?.experiences)
   99:         ? usage.experiences.map((item) => item.id).filter(Boolean)
  100:         : [],
  101:     },
  102:   ];
  103: 
  104:   return error;
  105: }
  106: 
  107: function sortSkillLibrary(skills) {
  108:   return [...skills].sort((firstSkill, secondSkill) =>
  109:     firstSkill.name.localeCompare(secondSkill.name, undefined, {
  110:       sensitivity: "base",
  111:     }),
  112:   );
  113: }

  629:       ).length,
  630:     }),
  631:     [skills, activeSkills, archivedSkills],
  632:   );
  633: 
  634:   const contextValue = useMemo(
  635:     () => ({
  636:       skills,
  637:       activeSkills,
  638:       archivedSkills,
  639:       skillsById,
  640:       skillsByCategory,
  641:       statistics,
  642: 
  643:       isLoading,
  644:       loadStatus,
  645:       saveStatus,
  646:       operation,
  647:       error,
  648: 
  649:       refreshSkills,
  650:       getSkillById,
  651:       resolveSkillMatch,
  652: 
  653:       createSkill,
  654:       updateSkill,
  655:       findOrCreateSkill,
  656:       archiveSkill,
  657:       restoreSkill,
  658:       deleteSkill,
  659:       replaceSkills,
  660: 
  661:       validateSkillDraft,
  662:       checkSkillNameAvailability,
  663: 
  664:       clearError,
  665:       resetSaveStatus,
  666:       resetOperation,
  667:     }),
  668:     [
  669:       skills,
  670:       activeSkills,
  671:       archivedSkills,
  672:       skillsById,
  673:       skillsByCategory,
  674:       statistics,
  675:       isLoading,
  676:       loadStatus,
  677:       saveStatus,
  678:       operation,
  679:       error,
  680:       refreshSkills,
  681:       getSkillById,
  682:       resolveSkillMatch,
  683:       createSkill,
  684:       updateSkill,
  685:       findOrCreateSkill,
  686:       archiveSkill,
  687:       restoreSkill,
  688:       deleteSkill,
  689:       replaceSkills,
  690:       validateSkillDraft,
  691:       checkSkillNameAvailability,
  692:       clearError,
  693:       resetSaveStatus,
  694:       resetOperation,
  695:     ],
  696:   );
  697: 
  698:   return (
  699:     <SkillDataContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
  700:       {children}
  701:     </SkillDataContext.Provider>
  702:   );
  703: }
  704: 
  705: export function useSkillData() {
  706:   const context = useContext(SkillDataContext);
  707: 
  708:   if (!context) {
  709:     throw new Error("useSkillData must be used inside SkillDataProvider.");
  710:   }
  711: 
  712:   return context;
  713: }

   33: function createSkillServiceError({
   34:   message,
   35:   publicMessage,
   36:   code,
   37:   status = 500,
   38:   details = null,
   39:   conflicts = [],
   40:   usage = null,
   41: }) {
   42:   const error = new Error(message);
   43: 
   44:   error.name = "SkillServiceError";
   45:   error.code = code;
   46:   error.status = status;
   47:   error.publicMessage = publicMessage;
   48:   error.details = details;
   49: 
   50:   error.conflicts = Array.isArray(conflicts) ? conflicts : [];
   51: 
   52:   error.usage = usage && typeof usage === "object" ? usage : null;
   53: 
   54:   return error;
   55: }

   69: /*
   70:  * =========================================
   71:  * Skill Usage Conflict
   72:  * =========================================
   73:  */
   74: 
   75: function createSkillUsageConflict(skill, usage) {
   76:   const experienceCount = Number(usage?.experienceCount) || 0;
   77: 
   78:   const educationCount = Number(usage?.educationCount) || 0;
   79: 
   80:   const trainingCount = Number(usage?.trainingCount) || 0;
   81: 
   82:   const resumeCount = Number(usage?.resumeCount) || 0;
   83: 
   84:   const portfolioCount = Number(usage?.portfolioCount) || 0;
   85: 
   86:   const experienceSkillCount = Number(usage?.experienceSkillCount) || 0;
   87: 
   88:   const experienceTechnologyCount =
   89:     Number(usage?.experienceTechnologyCount) || 0;
   90: 
   91:   const educationSkillCount = Number(usage?.educationSkillCount) || 0;
   92: 
   93:   const trainingSkillCount = Number(usage?.trainingSkillCount) || 0;
   94: 
   95:   const relationshipCount =
   96:     experienceSkillCount +
   97:     experienceTechnologyCount +
   98:     educationSkillCount +
   99:     trainingSkillCount;
  100: 
  101:   const recordCount =
  102:     experienceCount +
  103:     educationCount +
  104:     trainingCount +
  105:     resumeCount +
  106:     portfolioCount;
  107: 
  108:   const publicMessage =
  109:     `"${skill.name}" cannot be permanently deleted because it is still ` +
  110:     `referenced by ${recordCount} ${
  111:       recordCount === 1 ? "saved record" : "saved records"
  112:     }. Archive it instead.`;
  113: 
  114:   const experienceConflicts = Array.isArray(usage?.experiences)
  115:     ? usage.experiences.map((experience) => ({
  116:         type: "experience",
  117: 
  118:         recordId: experience.id,
  119: 
  120:         recordName:
  121:           `${experience.positionTitle} at ` + experience.organizationName,
  122: 
  123:         positionTitle: experience.positionTitle,
  124: 
  125:         organizationName: experience.organizationName,
  126: 
  127:         usedAsSkill: experience.usedAsSkill === true,
  128: 
  129:         usedAsTechnology: experience.usedAsTechnology === true,
  130:       }))
  131:     : [];
  132: 
  133:   const educationConflicts = Array.isArray(usage?.educationRecords)
  134:     ? usage.educationRecords.map((education) => ({
  135:         type: "education",
  136: 
  137:         recordId: education.id,
  138: 
  139:         recordName:
  140:           `${education.credentialName} at ` + education.institutionName,
  141: 
  142:         credentialName: education.credentialName,
  143: 
  144:         institutionName: education.institutionName,
  145:       }))
  146:     : [];
  147: 
  148:   const trainingConflicts = Array.isArray(usage?.trainingRecords)
  149:     ? usage.trainingRecords.map((training) => ({
  150:         type: "training",
  151: 
  152:         recordId: training.id,
  153: 
  154:         recordName: `${training.title} from ` + training.providerName,
  155: 
  156:         title: training.title,
  157: 
  158:         providerName: training.providerName,
  159:       }))
  160:     : [];
  161: 
  162:   return createSkillServiceError({
  163:     message:
  164:       `Skill "${skill.id}" has stored relationships ` +
  165:       "and cannot be deleted.",
  166: 
  167:     publicMessage,
  168: 
  169:     code: "SKILL_IN_USE",
  170: 
  171:     status: 409,
  172: 
  173:     details: {
  174:       skillId: skill.id,
  175:       skillName: skill.name,
  176: 
  177:       recordCount,
  178:       relationshipCount,
  179: 
  180:       experienceCount,
  181:       educationCount,
  182:       trainingCount,
  183:       resumeCount,
  184:       portfolioCount,
  185: 
  186:       experienceSkillCount,
  187:       experienceTechnologyCount,
  188:       educationSkillCount,
  189:       trainingSkillCount,
  190:     },
  191: 
  192:     usage,
  193: 
  194:     conflicts: [
  195:       ...experienceConflicts,
  196:       ...educationConflicts,
  197:       ...trainingConflicts,
  198:     ],
  199:   });
  200: }

  202: /*
  203:  * =========================================
  204:  * Candidate Creation
  205:  * =========================================
  206:  */
  207: 
  208: function createSkillCandidate(skillData = {}) {
  209:   const defaults = createEmptySkill();
  210: 
  211:   const source = skillData && typeof skillData === "object" ? skillData : {};
  212: 
  213:   return {
  214:     ...defaults,
  215:     ...source,
  216: 
  217:     proficiency: {
  218:       ...defaults.proficiency,
  219:       ...source.proficiency,
  220:     },
  221: 
  222:     language: {
  223:       ...defaults.language,
  224:       ...source.language,
  225:     },
  226:   };
  227: }

  229: /*
  230:  * =========================================
  231:  * Get All Skills
  232:  * =========================================
  233:  */
  234: 
  235: export async function getSkills({
  236:   includeArchived = false,
  237:   category = "all",
  238:   type = "all",
  239:   query = "",
  240: } = {}) {
  241:   const skills = readStoredSkills();
  242: 
  243:   const normalizedQuery = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(query);
  244: 
  245:   return skills
  246:     .filter((skill) => {
  247:       if (!includeArchived && skill.status === "archived") {
  248:         return false;
  249:       }
  250: 
  251:       if (category !== "all" && skill.category !== category) {
  252:         return false;
  253:       }
  254: 
  255:       if (type !== "all" && skill.type !== type) {
  256:         return false;
  257:       }
  258: 
  259:       if (!normalizedQuery) {
  260:         return true;
  261:       }
  262: 
  263:       const searchableValues = [
  264:         skill.name,
  265:         skill.description,
  266:         skill.category,
  267:         skill.type,
  268:         ...(skill.aliases || []),
  269:       ]
  270:         .map(normalizeSkillNameForComparison)
  271:         .filter(Boolean);
  272: 
  273:       return searchableValues.some((value) => value.includes(normalizedQuery));
  274:     })
  275:     .sort((firstSkill, secondSkill) =>
  276:       firstSkill.name.localeCompare(secondSkill.name, undefined, {
  277:         sensitivity: "base",
  278:       }),
  279:     );
  280: }

  283:  * =========================================
  284:  * Get Skill By ID
  285:  * =========================================
  286:  */
  287: 
  288: export async function getSkillById(skillId) {
  289:   if (!skillId) {
  290:     throw createSkillServiceError({
  291:       message: "A skill ID is required to retrieve a skill.",
  292: 
  293:       publicMessage: "The requested skill could not be identified.",
  294: 
  295:       code: "SKILL_ID_REQUIRED",
  296: 
  297:       status: 400,
  298:     });
  299:   }
  300: 
  301:   const skills = readStoredSkills();
  302: 
  303:   const skill = skills.find((candidate) => candidate.id === skillId);
  304: 
  305:   if (!skill) {
  306:     throw createSkillNotFoundError(skillId);
  307:   }
  308: 
  309:   return skill;
  310: }

  312: /*
  313:  * =========================================
  314:  * Find Skill By Name or Alias
  315:  * =========================================
  316:  */
  317: 
  318: export async function findSkillByName(name) {
  319:   if (!normalizeSkillNameForComparison(name)) {
  320:     return null;
  321:   }
  322: 
  323:   return findMatchingSkill(readStoredSkills(), name);
  324: }

  326: /*
  327:  * =========================================
  328:  * Create Skill
  329:  * =========================================
  330:  */
  331: 
  332: export async function createSkill(skillData) {
  333:   const skills = readStoredSkills();
  334: 
  335:   const candidate = createSkillCandidate(skillData);
  336: 
  337:   assertValidSkill(candidate, {
  338:     existingSkills: skills,
  339:   });
  340: 
  341:   const skill = createSkillModel(candidate);
  342: 
  343:   const duplicateId = skills.some(
  344:     (existingSkill) => existingSkill.id === skill.id,
  345:   );
  346: 
  347:   if (duplicateId) {
  348:     throw createSkillServiceError({
  349:       message: `Skill ID "${skill.id}" already exists.`,
  350: 
  351:       publicMessage:
  352:         "This skill could not be created because its identifier already exists.",
  353: 
  354:       code: "SKILL_ID_CONFLICT",
  355: 
  356:       status: 409,
  357:     });
  358:   }
  359: 
  360:   replaceStoredSkills([skill, ...skills]);
  361: 
  362:   return skill;
  363: }
  364: 
  365: /*
  366:  * =========================================
  367:  * Update Skill
  368:  * =========================================
  369:  */
  370: 
  371: export async function updateSkill(skillId, updates = {}) {
  372:   if (!skillId) {
  373:     throw createSkillServiceError({
  374:       message: "A skill ID is required to update a skill.",
  375: 
  376:       publicMessage: "The skill could not be identified.",
  377: 
  378:       code: "SKILL_ID_REQUIRED",
  379: 
  380:       status: 400,
  381:     });
  382:   }
  383: 
  384:   const skills = readStoredSkills();
  385: 
  386:   const skillIndex = skills.findIndex((skill) => skill.id === skillId);
  387: 
  388:   if (skillIndex === -1) {
  389:     throw createSkillNotFoundError(skillId);
  390:   }
  391: 
  392:   const currentSkill = skills[skillIndex];
  393: 
  394:   const safeUpdates = updates && typeof updates === "object" ? updates : {};
  395: 
  396:   const mergedSkill = {
  397:     ...currentSkill,
  398:     ...safeUpdates,
  399: 
  400:     proficiency: {
  401:       ...currentSkill.proficiency,
  402:       ...safeUpdates.proficiency,
  403:     },
  404: 
  405:     language: {
  406:       ...currentSkill.language,
  407:       ...safeUpdates.language,
  408:     },
  409: 
  410:     id: currentSkill.id,
  411: 
  412:     createdAt: currentSkill.createdAt,
  413:   };
  414: 
  415:   assertValidSkill(mergedSkill, {
  416:     existingSkills: skills,
  417:     excludeSkillId: skillId,
  418:   });
  419: 
  420:   const updatedSkill = updateSkillModel(currentSkill, safeUpdates);
  421: 
  422:   const nextSkills = [...skills];
  423: 
  424:   nextSkills[skillIndex] = updatedSkill;
  425: 
  426:   replaceStoredSkills(nextSkills);
  427: 
  428:   return updatedSkill;
  429: }
  430: 
  431: /*
  432:  * =========================================
  433:  * Archive Skill
  434:  * =========================================
  435:  */
  436: 
  437: export async function archiveSkill(skillId) {
  438:   /*
  439:    * Archiving is allowed even when the skill is
  440:    * referenced. Experience, Education, and Training
  441:    * relationships preserve readable snapshots.
  442:    */
  443: 
  444:   return updateSkill(skillId, {
  445:     status: "archived",
  446:   });
  447: }
  448: 
  449: /*
  450:  * =========================================
  451:  * Restore Skill
  452:  * =========================================
  453:  */
  454: 
  455: export async function restoreSkill(skillId) {
  456:   return updateSkill(skillId, {
  457:     status: "active",
  458:   });
  459: }
  460: 
  461: /*
  462:  * =========================================
  463:  * Resolve Stored Usage
  464:  * =========================================
  465:  */
  466: 
  467: function resolveStoredSkillUsage(skillId) {
  468:   /*
  469:    * Active and archived parent records are checked.
  470:    * Archiving a parent record does not remove its
  471:    * central Skill Library relationships.
  472:    */
  473: 
  474:   const experiences = readStoredExperiences();
  475: 
  476:   const educationRecords = readStoredEducation();
  477: 
  478:   const trainingRecords = readStoredTraining();
  479: 
  480:   return getSkillUsage(skillId, {
  481:     experiences,
  482:     educationRecords,
  483:     trainingRecords,
  484:   });
  485: }
  486: 
  487: /*
  488:  * =========================================
  489:  * Delete Skill
  490:  * =========================================
  491:  */
  492: 
  493: export async function deleteSkill(skillId) {
  494:   if (!skillId) {
  495:     throw createSkillServiceError({
  496:       message: "A skill ID is required to delete a skill.",
  497: 
  498:       publicMessage: "The skill could not be identified.",
  499: 
  500:       code: "SKILL_ID_REQUIRED",
  501: 
  502:       status: 400,
  503:     });
  504:   }
  505: 
  506:   const skills = readStoredSkills();
  507: 
  508:   const skillToDelete = skills.find((skill) => skill.id === skillId);
  509: 
  510:   if (!skillToDelete) {
  511:     throw createSkillNotFoundError(skillId);
  512:   }
  513: 
  514:   /*
  515:  * The service independently reads Experience,
  516:  * Education, and Training storage. Deletion
  517:  * protection cannot be bypassed by calling
  518:  * skillService directly.
  519:  */
  520: 
  521:   const usage = resolveStoredSkillUsage(skillId);
  522: 
  523:   if (Number(usage.total ?? usage.totalUsage) > 0) {
  524:     throw createSkillUsageConflict(skillToDelete, usage);
  525:   }
  526: 
  527:   const remainingSkills = skills.filter((skill) => skill.id !== skillId);
  528: 
  529:   replaceStoredSkills(remainingSkills);
  530: 
  531:   return {
  532:     id: skillId,
  533: 
  534:     name: skillToDelete.name,
  535: 
  536:     deleted: true,
  537:   };
  538: }
  539: 
  540: /*
  541:  * =========================================
  542:  * Resolve Skill Match
  543:  * =========================================
  544:  */
  545: 
  546: export async function resolveSkillMatch(name) {
  547:   const skills = readStoredSkills();
  548: 
  549:   const availability = validateSkillNameAvailability(name, skills);
  550: 
  551:   if (availability.isAvailable || !availability.matchingSkill) {
  552:     return {
  553:       matched: false,
  554:       matchedBy: "",
  555:       skill: null,
  556:     };
  557:   }
  558: 
  559:   const matchingSkill = availability.matchingSkill;
  560: 
  561:   const normalizedRequestedName = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(name);
  562: 
  563:   const normalizedPrimaryName = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(
  564:     matchingSkill.name,
  565:   );
  566: 
  567:   return {
  568:     matched: true,
  569: 
  570:     matchedBy:
  571:       normalizedRequestedName === normalizedPrimaryName ? "name" : "alias",
  572: 
  573:     skill: matchingSkill,
  574:   };
  575: }
  576: 
  577: /*
  578:  * =========================================
  579:  * Find or Create Skill
  580:  * =========================================
  581:  */
  582: 
  583: export async function findOrCreateSkill(
  584:   skillData,
  585:   { restoreArchived = true } = {},
  586: ) {
  587:   const candidateData =
  588:     typeof skillData === "string"
  589:       ? {
  590:           name: skillData,
  591:         }
  592:       : skillData && typeof skillData === "object"
  593:         ? skillData
  594:         : {};
  595: 
  596:   const name =
  597:     typeof candidateData.name === "string" ? candidateData.name.trim() : "";
  598: 
  599:   if (!name) {
  600:     throw createSkillServiceError({
  601:       message: "A skill name is required for find-or-create.",
  602: 
  603:       publicMessage: "Enter a skill name before adding it.",
  604: 
  605:       code: "SKILL_NAME_REQUIRED",
  606: 
  607:       status: 400,
  608:     });
  609:   }
  610: 
  611:   const matchResult = await resolveSkillMatch(name);
  612: 
  613:   if (matchResult.matched) {
  614:     let matchedSkill = matchResult.skill;
  615: 
  616:     let restored = false;
  617: 
  618:     if (restoreArchived && matchedSkill.status === "archived") {
  619:       matchedSkill = await restoreSkill(matchedSkill.id);
  620: 
  621:       restored = true;
  622:     }
  623: 
  624:     return {
  625:       skill: matchedSkill,
  626: 
  627:       created: false,
  628: 
  629:       restored,
  630: 
  631:       matched: true,
  632: 
  633:       matchedBy: matchResult.matchedBy,
  634:     };
  635:   }
  636: 
  637:   const createdSkill = await createSkill({
  638:     ...candidateData,
  639: 
  640:     name,
  641: 
  642:     source: candidateData.source || "manual",
  643: 
  644:     sourceContext: candidateData.sourceContext || "experience",
  645:   });
  646: 
  647:   return {
  648:     skill: createdSkill,
  649: 
  650:     created: true,
  651: 
  652:     restored: false,
  653: 
  654:     matched: false,
  655: 
  656:     matchedBy: "",
  657:   };
  658: }
  659: 
  660: /*
  661:  * =========================================
  662:  * Validate Skill Draft
  663:  * =========================================
  664:  */
  665: 
  666: export async function validateSkillDraft(
  667:   skillData,
  668:   { excludeSkillId = "" } = {},
  669: ) {
  670:   const existingSkills = readStoredSkills();
  671: 
  672:   const candidate = createSkillCandidate(skillData);
  673: 
  674:   return validateSkill(candidate, {
  675:     existingSkills,
  676:     excludeSkillId,
  677:   });
  678: }
  679: 
  680: /*
  681:  * =========================================
  682:  * Validate Name Availability
  683:  * =========================================
  684:  */
  685: 
  686: export async function checkSkillNameAvailability(
  687:   name,
  688:   { excludeSkillId = "" } = {},
  689: ) {
  690:   return validateSkillNameAvailability(
  691:     name,
  692:     readStoredSkills(),
  693:     excludeSkillId,
  694:   );
  695: }
  696: 
  697: /*
  698:  * =========================================
  699:  * Replace Skill Library
  700:  * =========================================
  701:  */
  702: 
  703: export async function replaceSkills(skillValues) {
  704:   if (!Array.isArray(skillValues)) {
  705:     throw createSkillServiceError({
  706:       message: "The replacement Skill Library must be an array.",
  707: 
  708:       publicMessage: "The Skill Library has an invalid format.",
  709: 
  710:       code: "INVALID_SKILL_COLLECTION",
  711: 
  712:       status: 400,
  713:     });
  714:   }
  715: 
  716:   const validatedSkills = [];
  717: 
  718:   skillValues.forEach((skillValue, index) => {
  719:     const candidate = createSkillCandidate(skillValue);
  720: 
  721:     const otherSkills = skillValues.filter(
  722:       (_, candidateIndex) => candidateIndex !== index,
  723:     );
  724: 
  725:     assertValidSkill(candidate, {
  726:       existingSkills: otherSkills,
  727:       excludeSkillId: candidate.id,
  728:     });
  729: 
  730:     validatedSkills.push(normalizeSkill(candidate));
  731:   });
  732: 
  733:   const normalizedSkills = normalizeSkillCollection(validatedSkills);
  734: 
  735:   return replaceStoredSkills(normalizedSkills);
  736: }

    6: /*
    7:  * =========================================
    8:  * Storage Configuration
    9:  * =========================================
   10:  */
   11: 
   12: export const SKILL_STORAGE_KEY = "portfolio-platform:skills";
   13: 
   14: const SKILL_STORAGE_VERSION = 1;
   15: 
   16: /*
   17:  * =========================================
   18:  * Storage Error
   19:  * =========================================
   20:  */
   21: 
   22: function createSkillStorageError(message, publicMessage, cause) {
   23:   const error = new Error(message);
   24: 
   25:   error.name = "SkillStorageError";
   26:   error.code = "SKILL_STORAGE_ERROR";
   27:   error.publicMessage = publicMessage;
   28: 
   29:   if (cause) {
   30:     error.cause = cause;
   31:   }
   32: 
   33:   return error;
   34: }
   35: 
   36: /*
   37:  * =========================================
   38:  * Browser Storage
   39:  * =========================================
   40:  */
   41: 
   42: function getBrowserStorage() {
   43:   if (typeof window === "undefined") {
   44:     throw createSkillStorageError(
   45:       "Skill storage is unavailable outside the browser.",
   46:       "Skill storage is currently unavailable.",
   47:     );
   48:   }
   49: 
   50:   if (!window.localStorage) {
   51:     throw createSkillStorageError(
   52:       "The browser does not provide local storage.",
   53:       "Your browser does not support local skill storage.",
   54:     );
   55:   }
   56: 
   57:   return window.localStorage;
   58: }
   59: 
   60: /*
   61:  * =========================================
   62:  * Empty Storage Document
   63:  * =========================================
   64:  */
   65: 
   66: function createEmptySkillStorageDocument() {
   67:   return {
   68:     storageVersion: SKILL_STORAGE_VERSION,
   69:     skillModelVersion: SKILL_MODEL_VERSION,
   70:     skills: [],
   71:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   72:   };
   73: }
   74: 
   75: /*
   76:  * =========================================
   77:  * Parse Storage Document
   78:  * =========================================
   79:  */
   80: 
   81: function parseSkillStorageDocument(value) {
   82:   if (!value) {
   83:     return createEmptySkillStorageDocument();
   84:   }
   85: 
   86:   let parsedValue;
   87: 
   88:   try {
   89:     parsedValue = JSON.parse(value);
   90:   } catch (cause) {
   91:     throw createSkillStorageError(
   92:       "Unable to parse stored skill data.",
   93:       "The saved Skill Library could not be read.",
   94:       cause,
   95:     );
   96:   }
   97: 
   98:   /*
   99:    * Support an older storage format where the
  100:    * collection was saved directly as an array.
  101:    */
  102: 
  103:   if (Array.isArray(parsedValue)) {
  104:     return {
  105:       ...createEmptySkillStorageDocument(),
  106: 
  107:       skills: normalizeSkillCollection(parsedValue),
  108:     };
  109:   }
  110: 
  111:   if (!parsedValue || typeof parsedValue !== "object") {
  112:     throw createSkillStorageError(
  113:       "The stored Skill Library has an invalid structure.",
  114:       "The saved Skill Library has an invalid format.",
  115:     );
  116:   }
  117: 
  118:   return {
  119:     storageVersion: Number(parsedValue.storageVersion) || SKILL_STORAGE_VERSION,
  120: 
  121:     skillModelVersion:
  122:       Number(parsedValue.skillModelVersion) || SKILL_MODEL_VERSION,
  123: 
  124:     skills: normalizeSkillCollection(parsedValue.skills),
  125: 
  126:     updatedAt:
  127:       typeof parsedValue.updatedAt === "string"
  128:         ? parsedValue.updatedAt
  129:         : new Date().toISOString(),
  130:   };
  131: }
  132: 
  133: /*
  134:  * =========================================
  135:  * Read Storage Document
  136:  * =========================================
  137:  */
  138: 
  139: export function readSkillStorage() {
  140:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
  141: 
  142:   try {
  143:     const storedValue = storage.getItem(SKILL_STORAGE_KEY);
  144: 
  145:     return parseSkillStorageDocument(storedValue);
  146:   } catch (error) {
  147:     if (error?.name === "SkillStorageError") {
  148:       throw error;
  149:     }
  150: 
  151:     throw createSkillStorageError(
  152:       "Unable to read the Skill Library.",
  153:       "Your saved skills could not be loaded.",
  154:       error,
  155:     );
  156:   }
  157: }
  158: 
  159: /*
  160:  * =========================================
  161:  * Write Storage Document
  162:  * =========================================
  163:  */
  164: 
  165: export function writeSkillStorage(skills) {
  166:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
  167: 
  168:   const storageDocument = {
  169:     storageVersion: SKILL_STORAGE_VERSION,
  170:     skillModelVersion: SKILL_MODEL_VERSION,
  171: 
  172:     skills: normalizeSkillCollection(skills),
  173: 
  174:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  175:   };
  176: 
  177:   try {
  178:     storage.setItem(SKILL_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(storageDocument));
  179: 
  180:     return storageDocument;
  181:   } catch (error) {
  182:     throw createSkillStorageError(
  183:       "Unable to write the Skill Library.",
  184:       "Your skills could not be saved in this browser.",
  185:       error,
  186:     );
  187:   }
  188: }
  189: 
  190: /*
  191:  * =========================================
  192:  * Read Skills
  193:  * =========================================
  194:  */
  195: 
  196: export function readStoredSkills() {
  197:   return readSkillStorage().skills;
  198: }
  199: 
  200: /*
  201:  * =========================================
  202:  * Replace Skills
  203:  * =========================================
  204:  */
  205: 
  206: export function replaceStoredSkills(skills) {
  207:   return writeSkillStorage(skills).skills;
  208: }
  209: 
  210: /*
  211:  * =========================================
  212:  * Update Skills Transaction
  213:  * =========================================
  214:  *
  215:  * This reads the current collection, applies an
  216:  * updater, and saves the returned collection.
  217:  */
  218: 
  219: export function updateStoredSkills(updater) {
  220:   if (typeof updater !== "function") {
  221:     throw createSkillStorageError(
  222:       "The Skill Library updater must be a function.",
  223:       "The Skill Library could not be updated.",
  224:     );
  225:   }
  226: 
  227:   const currentSkills = readStoredSkills();
  228: 
  229:   const nextSkills = updater([...currentSkills]);
  230: 
  231:   if (!Array.isArray(nextSkills)) {
  232:     throw createSkillStorageError(
  233:       "The Skill Library updater did not return an array.",
  234:       "The Skill Library could not be updated.",
  235:     );
  236:   }
  237: 
  238:   return replaceStoredSkills(nextSkills);
  239: }
  240: 
  241: /*
  242:  * =========================================
  243:  * Clear Skill Storage
  244:  * =========================================
  245:  *
  246:  * This removes the entire local Skill Library.
  247:  * Normal delete operations should remove only one
  248:  * skill through skillService.js.
  249:  */
  250: 
  251: export function clearSkillStorage() {
  252:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
  253: 
  254:   try {
  255:     storage.removeItem(SKILL_STORAGE_KEY);
  256:   } catch (error) {
  257:     throw createSkillStorageError(
  258:       "Unable to clear the Skill Library.",
  259:       "The saved Skill Library could not be cleared.",
  260:       error,
  261:     );
  262:   }
  263: }
  264: 
  265: /*
  266:  * =========================================
  267:  * Storage Information
  268:  * =========================================
  269:  */
  270: 
  271: export function getSkillStorageInformation() {
  272:   const document = readSkillStorage();
  273: 
  274:   return {
  275:     storageKey: SKILL_STORAGE_KEY,
  276:     storageVersion: document.storageVersion,
  277: 
  278:     skillModelVersion: document.skillModelVersion,
  279: 
  280:     skillCount: document.skills.length,
  281: 
  282:     updatedAt: document.updatedAt,
  283:   };
  284: }

  106: export function getSkillMetadata(skill) {

  108:

  114:

  122: /*
  123:  * =========================================
  124:  * Last Used Date
  125:  * =========================================
  126:  */
  127: 
  128: export function formatSkillLastUsedDate(
  129:   value,
  130:   { locale = "en-US", fallback = "" } = {},
  131: ) {
  132:   const normalizedValue = getText(value);
  133: 
  134:   if (!/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/.test(normalizedValue)) {
  135:     return fallback;
  136:   }
  137: 
  138:   const [year, month] = normalizedValue.split("-").map(Number);
  139: 
  140:   const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, 1));
  141: 
  142:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, {
  143:     month: "short",
  144:     year: "numeric",
  145:     timeZone: "UTC",
  146:   }).format(date);
  147: }

  149: /*
  150:  * =========================================
  151:  * Search
  152:  * =========================================
  153:  */
  154: 
  155: export function createSkillSearchText(skill) {
  156:   return [
  157:     skill?.name,
  158:     skill?.description,
  159:     skill?.category,
  160:     skill?.type,
  161:     skill?.source,
  162:     skill?.sourceContext,
  163:     skill?.notes,
  164:     skill?.proficiency?.level,
  165:     skill?.language?.proficiency,
  166:     getSkillCategoryDisplay(skill),
  167:     getSkillTypeDisplay(skill),
  168:     getSkillProficiencyDisplay(skill),
  169:     ...getArray(skill?.aliases),
  170:   ]
  171:     .map(getText)
  172:     .filter(Boolean)
  173:     .join(" ")
  174:     .normalize("NFKC")
  175:     .toLocaleLowerCase();
  176: }
  177: 
  178: export function searchSkills(skills = [], query = "") {
  179:   const normalizedQuery = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(query);
  180: 
  181:   if (!normalizedQuery) {
  182:     return [...getArray(skills)];
  183:   }
  184: 
  185:   const terms = normalizedQuery.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  186: 
  187:   return getArray(skills).filter((skill) => {
  188:     const searchableText = createSkillSearchText(skill);
  189: 
  190:     return terms.every((term) => searchableText.includes(term));
  191:   });
  192: }
  193: 
  194: /*
  195:  * =========================================
  196:  * Filtering
  197:  * =========================================
  198:  */
  199: 
  200: export function filterSkills(skills = [], filters = {}) {
  201:   const {
  202:     category = "all",
  203:     type = "all",
  204:     proficiency = "all",
  205:     source = "all",
  206:     status = "all",
  207:   } = filters;
  208: 
  209:   return getArray(skills).filter((skill) => {
  210:     if (category !== "all" && skill?.category !== category) {
  211:       return false;
  212:     }
  213: 
  214:     if (type !== "all" && skill?.type !== type) {
  215:       return false;
  216:     }
  217: 
  218:     if (proficiency !== "all" && skill?.proficiency?.level !== proficiency) {
  219:       return false;
  220:     }
  221: 
  222:     if (source !== "all" && skill?.source !== source) {
  223:       return false;
  224:     }
  225: 
  226:     if (status !== "all" && skill?.status !== status) {
  227:       return false;
  228:     }
  229: 
  230:     return true;
  231:   });
  232: }
  233: 
  234: /*
  235:  * =========================================
  236:  * Sorting
  237:  * =========================================
  238:  */
  239: 
  240: function compareText(firstValue, secondValue) {
  241:   return getText(firstValue).localeCompare(getText(secondValue), undefined, {
  242:     sensitivity: "base",
  243:   });
  244: }
  245: 
  246: function getUpdatedTime(skill) {
  247:   const timestamp = new Date(skill?.updatedAt || 0).getTime();
  248: 
  249:   return Number.isFinite(timestamp) ? timestamp : 0;
  250: }
  251: 
  252: function getCreatedTime(skill) {
  253:   const timestamp = new Date(skill?.createdAt || 0).getTime();
  254: 
  255:   return Number.isFinite(timestamp) ? timestamp : 0;
  256: }
  257: 
  258: export function sortSkills(skills = [], sortOption = "name-ascending") {
  259:   const sortedSkills = [...getArray(skills)];
  260: 
  261:   sortedSkills.sort((firstSkill, secondSkill) => {
  262:     switch (sortOption) {
  263:       case "name-descending":
  264:         return compareText(secondSkill?.name, firstSkill?.name);
  265: 
  266:       case "category-ascending":
  267:         return (
  268:           compareText(
  269:             getSkillCategoryDisplay(firstSkill),
  270:             getSkillCategoryDisplay(secondSkill),
  271:           ) || compareText(firstSkill?.name, secondSkill?.name)
  272:         );
  273: 
  274:       case "type-ascending":
  275:         return (
  276:           compareText(
  277:             getSkillTypeDisplay(firstSkill),
  278:             getSkillTypeDisplay(secondSkill),
  279:           ) || compareText(firstSkill?.name, secondSkill?.name)
  280:         );
  281: 
  282:       case "proficiency-descending": {
  283:         const proficiencyOrder = {
  284:           expert: 4,
  285:           advanced: 3,
  286:           intermediate: 2,
  287:           foundational: 1,
  288:           "": 0,
  289:         };
  290: 
  291:         const firstLevel =
  292:           proficiencyOrder[firstSkill?.proficiency?.level || ""] || 0;
  293: 
  294:         const secondLevel =
  295:           proficiencyOrder[secondSkill?.proficiency?.level || ""] || 0;
  296: 
  297:         return (
  298:           secondLevel - firstLevel ||
  299:           compareText(firstSkill?.name, secondSkill?.name)
  300:         );
  301:       }
  302: 
  303:       case "years-descending":
  304:         return (
  305:           getNumericValue(secondSkill?.proficiency?.yearsOfExperience) -
  306:             getNumericValue(firstSkill?.proficiency?.yearsOfExperience) ||
  307:           compareText(firstSkill?.name, secondSkill?.name)
  308:         );
  309: 
  310:       case "recently-created":
  311:         return getCreatedTime(secondSkill) - getCreatedTime(firstSkill);
  312: 
  313:       case "recently-updated":
  314:         return getUpdatedTime(secondSkill) - getUpdatedTime(firstSkill);
  315: 
  316:       case "name-ascending":
  317:       default:
  318:         return compareText(firstSkill?.name, secondSkill?.name);
  319:     }
  320:   });
  321: 
  322:   return sortedSkills;
  323: }
  324: 
  325: export function prepareSkillCollection({
  326:   skills = [],
  327:   query = "",
  328:   filters = {},
  329:   sortOption = "name-ascending",
  330: } = {}) {
  331:   return sortSkills(
  332:     filterSkills(searchSkills(skills, query), filters),
  333:     sortOption,
  334:   );
  335: }
  336: 
  337: /*
  338:  * =========================================
  339:  * Grouping
  340:  * =========================================
  341:  */
  342: 
  343: export function groupSkillsByCategory(skills = []) {
  344:   return getArray(skills).reduce((groups, skill) => {
  345:     const category = skill?.category || "other";
  346: 
  347:     if (!groups[category]) {
  348:       groups[category] = [];
  349:     }
  350: 
  351:     groups[category].push(skill);
  352: 
  353:     return groups;
  354:   }, {});
  355: }
  356: 
  357: /*
  358:  * =========================================
  359:  * Completeness
  360:  * =========================================
  361:  */
  362: 
  363: export function getSkillCompleteness(skill) {
  364:   const isLanguage = skill?.type === "language";
  365: 
  366:   const checks = [
  367:     {
  368:       id: "name",
  369:       label: "Skill name",
  370:       complete: Boolean(getText(skill?.name)),
  371:       weight: 3,
  372:     },
  373:     {
  374:       id: "category",
  375:       label: "Category",
  376:       complete: Boolean(getText(skill?.category) && skill.category !== "other"),
  377:       weight: 1,
  378:     },
  379:     {
  380:       id: "type",
  381:       label: "Skill type",
  382:       complete: Boolean(getText(skill?.type) && skill.type !== "other"),
  383:       weight: 1,
  384:     },
  385:     {
  386:       id: "description",
  387:       label: "Description",
  388:       complete: Boolean(getText(skill?.description)),
  389:       weight: 2,
  390:     },
  391:     {
  392:       id: "proficiency",
  393:       label: isLanguage ? "Language proficiency" : "Proficiency",
  394:       complete: isLanguage
  395:         ? Boolean(getText(skill?.language?.proficiency))
  396:         : Boolean(getText(skill?.proficiency?.level)),
  397:       weight: 1,
  398:     },
  399:     {
  400:       id: "experience",
  401:       label: "Experience information",
  402:       complete:
  403:         getNumericValue(skill?.proficiency?.yearsOfExperience, -1) >= 0 ||
  404:         Boolean(getText(skill?.proficiency?.lastUsedDate)),
  405:       weight: 1,
  406:     },
  407:   ];
  408: 
  409:   const maximumScore = checks.reduce((total, check) => total + check.weight, 0);
  410: 
  411:   const score = checks.reduce(
  412:     (total, check) => total + (check.complete ? check.weight : 0),
  413:     0,
  414:   );
  415: 
  416:   const percentage =
  417:     maximumScore > 0 ? Math.round((score / maximumScore) * 100) : 0;
  418: 
  419:   let level = "basic";
  420:   let label = "Basic";
  421: 
  422:   if (percentage >= 90) {
  423:     level = "excellent";
  424:     label = "Excellent";
  425:   } else if (percentage >= 70) {
  426:     level = "strong";
  427:     label = "Strong";
  428:   } else if (percentage >= 45) {
  429:     level = "developing";
  430:     label = "Developing";
  431:   }
  432: 
  433:   return {
  434:     score,
  435:     maximumScore,
  436:     percentage,
  437:     level,
  438:     label,
  439:     checks,
  440: 
  441:     completedChecks: checks.filter((check) => check.complete),
  442: 
  443:     missingChecks: checks.filter((check) => !check.complete),
  444:   };
  445: }
  446: /*
  447:  * =========================================
  448:  * Relationship Helpers
  449:  * =========================================
  450:  */
  451: 
  452: function getRelationshipSkillId(relationship) {
  453:   if (typeof relationship === "string") {
  454:     return relationship.trim();
  455:   }
  456: 
  457:   if (!relationship || typeof relationship !== "object") {
  458:     return "";
  459:   }
  460: 
  461:   return String(
  462:     relationship.skillId || relationship.profileSkillId || "",
  463:   ).trim();
  464: }
  465: 
  466: function countSkillRelationships(relationships, skillId) {
  467:   if (!skillId) {
  468:     return 0;
  469:   }
  470: 
  471:   return getArray(relationships).filter(
  472:     (relationship) => getRelationshipSkillId(relationship) === skillId,
  473:   ).length;
  474: }
  475: 
  476: function getExperienceLabels(experience) {
  477:   return {
  478:     positionTitle: getText(experience?.position?.title) || "Untitled Position",
  479: 
  480:     organizationName:
  481:       getText(experience?.organization?.name) || "Organization not provided",
  482:   };
  483: }
  484: 
  485: function getEducationLabels(education) {
  486:   return {
  487:     credentialName:
  488:       getText(education?.credential?.name) || "Unnamed Credential",
  489: 
  490:     institutionName:
  491:       getText(education?.institution?.name) || "Institution not provided",
  492:   };
  493: }
  494: 
  495: function getTrainingLabels(training) {
  496:   return {
  497:     title: getText(training?.title) || "Untitled Training",
  498: 
  499:     providerName:
  500:       getText(training?.provider?.name) || "Training provider not provided",
  501:   };
  502: }
  503: 
  504: /*
  505:  * =========================================
  506:  * Empty Usage
  507:  * =========================================
  508:  */
  509: 
  510: export function createEmptySkillUsage(skillId = "") {
  511:   return {
  512:     skillId,
  513: 
  514:     /*
  515:      * Unique parent records using this skill.
  516:      */
  517: 
  518:     total: 0,
  519:     totalUsage: 0,
  520: 
  521:     /*
  522:      * Unique parent-record totals.
  523:      */
  524: 
  525:     experienceCount: 0,
  526:     educationCount: 0,
  527:     trainingCount: 0,
  528:     resumeCount: 0,
  529:     portfolioCount: 0,
  530: 
  531:     /*
  532:      * Relationship totals.
  533:      */
  534: 
  535:     experienceSkillCount: 0,
  536:     experienceTechnologyCount: 0,
  537:     educationSkillCount: 0,
  538:     trainingSkillCount: 0,
  539: 
  540:     /*
  541:      * Referencing records.
  542:      */
  543: 
  544:     experiences: [],
  545:     educationRecords: [],
  546:     trainingRecords: [],
  547:     resumes: [],
  548:     portfolios: [],
  549: 
  550:     isUsed: false,
  551:   };
  552: }
  553: 
  554: /*
  555:  * =========================================
  556:  * Experience Usage
  557:  * =========================================
  558:  */
  559: 
  560: export function getSkillExperienceUsage(skillId, experiences = []) {
  561:   const result = {
  562:     experienceCount: 0,
  563:     experienceSkillCount: 0,
  564:     experienceTechnologyCount: 0,
  565:     experiences: [],
  566:   };
  567: 
  568:   if (!skillId) {
  569:     return result;
  570:   }
  571: 
  572:   getArray(experiences).forEach((experience) => {
  573:     const skillRelationshipCount = countSkillRelationships(
  574:       experience?.skills,
  575:       skillId,
  576:     );
  577: 
  578:     const technologyRelationshipCount = countSkillRelationships(
  579:       experience?.technologies,
  580:       skillId,
  581:     );
  582: 
  583:     const usedAsSkill = skillRelationshipCount > 0;
  584:     const usedAsTechnology = technologyRelationshipCount > 0;
  585: 
  586:     if (!usedAsSkill && !usedAsTechnology) {
  587:       return;
  588:     }
  589: 
  590:     const labels = getExperienceLabels(experience);
  591: 
  592:     result.experienceSkillCount += skillRelationshipCount;
  593:     result.experienceTechnologyCount += technologyRelationshipCount;
  594: 
  595:     result.experiences.push({
  596:       id: getText(experience?.id),
  597: 
  598:       positionTitle: labels.positionTitle,
  599:       organizationName: labels.organizationName,
  600: 
  601:       usedAsSkill,
  602:       usedAsTechnology,
  603:     });
  604:   });
  605: 
  606:   result.experienceCount = result.experiences.length;
  607: 
  608:   return result;
  609: }
  610: 
  611: /*
  612:  * =========================================
  613:  * Education Usage
  614:  * =========================================
  615:  */
  616: 
  617: export function getSkillEducationUsage(skillId, educationRecords = []) {
  618:   const result = {
  619:     educationCount: 0,
  620:     educationSkillCount: 0,
  621:     educationRecords: [],
  622:   };
  623: 
  624:   if (!skillId) {
  625:     return result;
  626:   }
  627: 
  628:   getArray(educationRecords).forEach((education) => {
  629:     const relationshipCount = countSkillRelationships(
  630:       education?.skillRelationships,
  631:       skillId,
  632:     );
  633: 
  634:     if (relationshipCount === 0) {
  635:       return;
  636:     }
  637: 
  638:     const labels = getEducationLabels(education);
  639: 
  640:     result.educationSkillCount += relationshipCount;
  641: 
  642:     result.educationRecords.push({
  643:       id: getText(education?.id),
  644: 
  645:       credentialName: labels.credentialName,
  646:       institutionName: labels.institutionName,
  647: 
  648:       relationshipCount,
  649:     });
  650:   });
  651: 
  652:   result.educationCount = result.educationRecords.length;
  653: 
  654:   return result;
  655: }
  656: 
  657: /*
  658:  * =========================================
  659:  * Training Usage
  660:  * =========================================
  661:  */
  662: 
  663: export function getSkillTrainingUsage(skillId, trainingRecords = []) {
  664:   const result = {
  665:     trainingCount: 0,
  666:     trainingSkillCount: 0,
  667:     trainingRecords: [],
  668:   };
  669: 
  670:   if (!skillId) {
  671:     return result;
  672:   }
  673: 
  674:   getArray(trainingRecords).forEach((training) => {
  675:     const relationshipCount = countSkillRelationships(
  676:       training?.skillRelationships,
  677:       skillId,
  678:     );
  679: 
  680:     if (relationshipCount === 0) {
  681:       return;
  682:     }
  683: 
  684:     const labels = getTrainingLabels(training);
  685: 
  686:     result.trainingSkillCount += relationshipCount;
  687: 
  688:     result.trainingRecords.push({
  689:       id: getText(training?.id),
  690: 
  691:       title: labels.title,
  692: 
  693:       providerName: labels.providerName,
  694: 
  695:       relationshipCount,
  696:     });
  697:   });
  698: 
  699:   result.trainingCount = result.trainingRecords.length;
  700: 
  701:   return result;
  702: }
  703: 
  704: /*
  705:  * =========================================
  706:  * Complete Skill Usage
  707:  * =========================================
  708:  */
  709: 
  710: export function getSkillUsage(
  711:   skillId,
  712:   {
  713:     experiences = [],
  714:     educationRecords = [],
  715:     trainingRecords = [],
  716:     portfolios = [],
  717:     resumes = [],
  718:   } = {},
  719: ) {
  720:   const usage = createEmptySkillUsage(skillId);
  721: 
  722:   const experienceUsage = getSkillExperienceUsage(skillId, experiences);
  723: 
  724:   const educationUsage = getSkillEducationUsage(skillId, educationRecords);
  725: 
  726:   const trainingUsage = getSkillTrainingUsage(skillId, trainingRecords);
  727: 
  728:   usage.experienceCount = experienceUsage.experienceCount;
  729: 
  730:   usage.experienceSkillCount = experienceUsage.experienceSkillCount;
  731: 
  732:   usage.experienceTechnologyCount = experienceUsage.experienceTechnologyCount;
  733: 
  734:   usage.experiences = experienceUsage.experiences;
  735: 
  736:   usage.educationCount = educationUsage.educationCount;
  737: 
  738:   usage.educationSkillCount = educationUsage.educationSkillCount;
  739: 
  740:   usage.educationRecords = educationUsage.educationRecords;
  741: 
  742:   usage.trainingCount = trainingUsage.trainingCount;
  743: 
  744:   usage.trainingSkillCount = trainingUsage.trainingSkillCount;
  745: 
  746:   usage.trainingRecords = trainingUsage.trainingRecords;
  747: 
  748:   /*
  749:    * Résumé and Portfolio relationship models
  750:    * have not been implemented yet.
  751:    */
  752: 
  753:   usage.resumeCount = 0;
  754:   usage.portfolioCount = 0;
  755: 
  756:   usage.resumes = [];
  757:   usage.portfolios = [];
  758: 
  759:   /*
  760:    * Total represents unique parent records,
  761:    * not the number of relationship rows.
  762:    */
  763: 
  764:   usage.total =
  765:     usage.experienceCount +
  766:     usage.educationCount +
  767:     usage.trainingCount +
  768:     usage.resumeCount +
  769:     usage.portfolioCount;
  770: 
  771:   usage.totalUsage = usage.total;
  772: 
  773:   usage.isUsed = usage.total > 0;
  774: 
  775:   void portfolios;
  776:   void resumes;
  777: 
  778:   return usage;
  779: }
  780: 
  781: /*
  782:  * =========================================
  783:  * Usage Map
  784:  * =========================================
  785:  */
  786: 
  787: export function createSkillUsageMap({
  788:   skills = [],
  789:   experiences = [],
  790:   educationRecords = [],
  791:   trainingRecords = [],
  792:   portfolios = [],
  793:   resumes = [],
  794: } = {}) {
  795:   return getArray(skills).reduce((usageMap, skill) => {
  796:     if (!skill?.id) {
  797:       return usageMap;
  798:     }
  799: 
  800:     usageMap[skill.id] = getSkillUsage(skill.id, {
  801:       experiences,
  802:       educationRecords,
  803:       trainingRecords,
  804:       portfolios,
  805:       resumes,
  806:     });
  807: 
  808:     return usageMap;
  809:   }, {});
  810: }
  811: 
  812: export function isSkillInUse(usage) {
  813:   return Number(usage?.total ?? usage?.totalUsage) > 0;
  814: }
  815: 
  816: /*
  817:  * =========================================
  818:  * Usage Summary
  819:  * =========================================
  820:  */
  821: 
  822: export function getSkillUsageSummary(usage) {
  823:   if (!isSkillInUse(usage)) {
  824:     return "Not currently used";
  825:   }
  826: 
  827:   const parts = [];
  828: 
  829:   const experienceCount = Number(usage?.experienceCount) || 0;
  830: 
  831:   const educationCount = Number(usage?.educationCount) || 0;
  832: 
  833:   const trainingCount = Number(usage?.trainingCount) || 0;
  834: 
  835:   const resumeCount = Number(usage?.resumeCount) || 0;
  836: 
  837:   const portfolioCount = Number(usage?.portfolioCount) || 0;
  838: 
  839:   if (experienceCount > 0) {
  840:     parts.push(
  841:       `${experienceCount} ${
  842:         experienceCount === 1 ? "experience" : "experiences"
  843:       }`,
  844:     );
  845:   }
  846: 
  847:   if (educationCount > 0) {
  848:     parts.push(
  849:       `${educationCount} education ${
  850:         educationCount === 1 ? "record" : "records"
  851:       }`,
  852:     );
  853:   }
  854: 
  855:   if (trainingCount > 0) {
  856:     parts.push(
  857:       `${trainingCount} training ${trainingCount === 1 ? "record" : "records"}`,
  858:     );
  859:   }
  860: 
  861:   if (resumeCount > 0) {
  862:     parts.push(`${resumeCount} ${resumeCount === 1 ? "résumé" : "résumés"}`);
  863:   }
  864: 
  865:   if (portfolioCount > 0) {
  866:     parts.push(
  867:       `${portfolioCount} ${portfolioCount === 1 ? "portfolio" : "portfolios"}`,
  868:     );
  869:   }
  870: 
  871:   return parts.join(" · ");
  872: }
  873: 
  874: /*
  875:  * =========================================
  876:  * Statistics
  877:  * =========================================
  878:  */
  879: 
  880: export function getSkillStatistics(skills = []) {
  881:   const collection = getArray(skills);
  882: 
  883:   const active = getActiveSkills(collection);
  884: 
  885:   const archived = getArchivedSkills(collection);
  886: 
  887:   return {
  888:     total: collection.length,
  889: 
  890:     active: active.length,
  891: 
  892:     archived: archived.length,
  893: 
  894:     technical: active.filter((skill) => skill.type === "technical").length,
  895: 
  896:     professional: active.filter((skill) => skill.type === "professional")
  897:       .length,
  898: 
  899:     languages: active.filter((skill) => skill.type === "language").length,
  900: 
  901:     tools: active.filter((skill) => skill.type === "tool").length,
  902: 
  903:     aiSuggested: active.filter((skill) => skill.source === "ai-suggested")
  904:       .length,
  905: 
  906:     withDescriptions: active.filter((skill) =>
  907:       Boolean(getText(skill.description)),
  908:     ).length,
  909: 
  910:     withProficiency: active.filter((skill) =>
  911:       Boolean(
  912:         getText(skill.proficiency?.level) ||
  913:         getText(skill.language?.proficiency),
  914:       ),
  915:     ).length,
  916: 
  917:     categoryCount: new Set(
  918:       active.map((skill) => skill.category).filter(Boolean),
  919:     ).size,
  920:   };
  921: }

  938: 
  939:     type: skill.type,
  940: 
  941:     typeLabel: getSkillTypeDisplay(skill),
  942: 
  943:     proficiency: skill.proficiency?.level || "",
  944:   }));
  945: }

   21: }

   24:   return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];

   33: function createIssue(field, message, code = "invalid") {
   34:   return {
   35:     field,
   36:     message,
   37:     code,
   38:   };
   39: }

   41: /*
   42:  * =========================================
   43:  * Validation Collector
   44:  * =========================================
   45:  */
   46: 
   47: function createValidationCollector() {
   48:   const errors = [];
   49:   const warnings = [];
   50:   const conflicts = [];
   51: 
   52:   return {
   53:     errors,
   54:     warnings,
   55:     conflicts,
   56: 
   57:     addError(field, message, code) {
   58:       errors.push(createIssue(field, message, code));
   59:     },
   60: 
   61:     addWarning(field, message, code = "warning") {
   62:       warnings.push(createIssue(field, message, code));
   63:     },
   64: 
   65:     addConflict(field, message, conflictingSkill, code = "skill_conflict") {
   66:       const conflict = {
   67:         ...createIssue(field, message, code),
   68: 
   69:         conflictingSkillId: conflictingSkill?.id || "",
   70: 
   71:         conflictingSkillName: conflictingSkill?.name || "",
   72:       };
   73: 
   74:       conflicts.push(conflict);
   75:       errors.push(conflict);
   76:     },
   77:   };
   78: }

   80: /*
   81:  * =========================================
   82:  * Text Length Validation
   83:  * =========================================
   84:  */
   85: 
   86: function validateTextLength(collector, field, value, maximumLength, label) {
   87:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
   88:     return;
   89:   }
   90: 
   91:   if (value.trim().length > maximumLength) {
   92:     collector.addError(
   93:       field,
   94:       `${label} cannot exceed ${maximumLength} characters.`,
   95:       "too_long",
   96:     );
   97:   }
   98: }
   99: 
  100: /*
  101:  * =========================================
  102:  * Year-Month Validation
  103:  * =========================================
  104:  */
  105: 
  106: const YEAR_MONTH_PATTERN = /^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/;
  107: 
  108: function isValidYearMonth(value) {
  109:   return YEAR_MONTH_PATTERN.test(getText(value));
  110: }
  111: 
  112: function getCurrentYearMonth() {
  113:   const currentDate = new Date();
  114: 
  115:   const year = currentDate.getUTCFullYear();
  116: 
  117:   const month = String(currentDate.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  118: 
  119:   return `${year}-${month}`;
  120: }

  122: /*
  123:  * =========================================
  124:  * Required Skill Name
  125:  * =========================================
  126:  */
  127: 
  128: function validateSkillName(collector, skill) {
  129:   const name = getText(skill.name);
  130: 
  131:   if (!name) {
  132:     collector.addError("name", "Enter a skill name.", "required");
  133: 
  134:     return;
  135:   }
  136: 
  137:   validateTextLength(
  138:     collector,
  139:     "name",
  140:     skill.name,
  141:     SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.name,
  142:     "Skill name",
  143:   );
  144: 
  145:   if (name.length < 2) {
  146:     collector.addError(
  147:       "name",
  148:       "Skill name must contain at least two characters.",
  149:       "too_short",
  150:     );
  151:   }
  152: 
  153:   if (!/[a-zA-Z0-9À-ž]/u.test(name)) {
  154:     collector.addError(
  155:       "name",
  156:       "Skill name must contain letters or numbers.",
  157:       "invalid_name",
  158:     );
  159:   }
  160: 
  161:   if (/^[\s.,;:!?()[\]{}'"`~@#$%^&*+=|\\/<>_-]+$/.test(name)) {
  162:     collector.addError(
  163:       "name",
  164:       "Enter a meaningful skill name.",
  165:       "invalid_name",
  166:     );
  167:   }
  168: }

  175: 
  176: function validateConfiguredOptions(collector, skill) {

  194:       "Select a valid proficiency level.",
  195:       "invalid_option",
  196:     );
  197:   }

  204:       "Select a valid language proficiency.",
  205:       "invalid_option",
  206:     );
  207:   }

  212:       "Select a valid skill source.",
  213:       "invalid_option",
  214:     );
  215:   }
  216: 
  217:   if (!isValidSkillStatus(skill.status)) {
  218:     collector.addError(
  219:       "status",
  220:       "Select a valid skill status.",
  221:       "invalid_option",
  222:     );
  223:   }
