  257: 
  258: /*
  259:  * =========================================
  260:  * Proficiency
  261:  * =========================================
  262:  */
  263: 
  264: function validateProficiency(collector, skill) {
  265:   const proficiency = getObject(skill.proficiency);
  266: 
  267:   const level = getText(proficiency.level);
  268: 
  269:   const yearsOfExperience = proficiency.yearsOfExperience;
  270: 
  271:   if (
  272:     yearsOfExperience !== "" &&
  273:     yearsOfExperience !== null &&
  274:     yearsOfExperience !== undefined
  275:   ) {
  276:     const numericYears = Number(yearsOfExperience);
  277: 
  278:     if (!Number.isFinite(numericYears) || numericYears < 0) {
  279:       collector.addError(
  280:         "proficiency.yearsOfExperience",
  281:         "Years of experience must be zero or greater.",
  282:         "invalid_number",
  283:       );
  284:     } else if (numericYears > SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.yearsOfExperience) {
  285:       collector.addError(
  286:         "proficiency.yearsOfExperience",
  287:         `Years of experience cannot exceed ${SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.yearsOfExperience}.`,
  288:         "invalid_number",
  289:       );
  290:     } else if (Math.round(numericYears * 10) !== numericYears * 10) {
  291:       collector.addError(
  292:         "proficiency.yearsOfExperience",
  293:         "Years of experience can contain no more than one decimal place.",
  294:         "invalid_precision",
  295:       );
  296:     }
  297:   }
  298: 
  299:   const lastUsedDate = getText(proficiency.lastUsedDate);
  300: 
  301:   if (lastUsedDate && !isValidYearMonth(lastUsedDate)) {
  302:     collector.addError(
  303:       "proficiency.lastUsedDate",
  304:       "Enter a valid last-used month and year.",
  305:       "invalid_date",
  306:     );
  307:   }
  308: 
  309:   if (
  310:     lastUsedDate &&
  311:     isValidYearMonth(lastUsedDate) &&
  312:     lastUsedDate > getCurrentYearMonth()
  313:   ) {
  314:     collector.addError(
  315:       "proficiency.lastUsedDate",
  316:       "The last-used date cannot be in the future.",
  317:       "future_date",
  318:     );
  319:   }
  320: 
  321:   if (
  322:     level === "expert" &&
  323:     (yearsOfExperience === "" ||
  324:       yearsOfExperience === null ||
  325:       yearsOfExperience === undefined)
  326:   ) {
  327:     collector.addWarning(
  328:       "proficiency.level",
  329:       "Consider adding supporting experience before describing this skill as expert.",
  330:       "expert_without_experience",
  331:     );
  332:   }
  333: 
  334:   if (
  335:     level === "expert" &&
  336:     Number(yearsOfExperience) > 0 &&
  337:     Number(yearsOfExperience) < 3
  338:   ) {
  339:     collector.addWarning(
  340:       "proficiency.level",
  341:       "Review whether expert accurately describes this skill with the supplied experience duration.",
  342:       "expert_level_review",
  343:     );
  344:   }
  345: }
  346: 
  347: /*
  348:  * =========================================
  349:  * Language Rules
  350:  * =========================================
  351:  */
  352: 
  353: function validateLanguageRules(collector, skill) {
  354:   const isLanguageType = skill.type === "language";
  355: 
  356:   const isLanguageCategory = skill.category === "language";
  357: 
  358:   const languageProficiency = getText(skill.language?.proficiency);
  359: 
  360:   if (isLanguageType && !isLanguageCategory) {
  361:     collector.addError(
  362:       "category",
  363:       'A language skill must use the "Languages" category.',
  364:       "language_category_mismatch",
  365:     );
  366:   }
  367: 
  368:   if (isLanguageCategory && !isLanguageType) {
  369:     collector.addError(
  370:       "type",
  371:       'A skill in the "Languages" category must use the "Language Skill" type.',
  372:       "language_type_mismatch",
  373:     );
  374:   }
  375: 
  376:   if (!isLanguageType && languageProficiency) {
  377:     collector.addError(
  378:       "language.proficiency",
  379:       "Language proficiency can only be assigned to a language skill.",
  380:       "unexpected_language_proficiency",
  381:     );
  382:   }
  383: 
  384:   if (isLanguageType && !languageProficiency) {
  385:     collector.addWarning(
  386:       "language.proficiency",
  387:       "Consider selecting a language proficiency level.",
  388:       "missing_language_proficiency",
  389:     );
  390:   }
  391: }
  392: 
  393: /*
  394:  * =========================================
  395:  * Aliases
  396:  * =========================================
  397:  */
  398: 
  399: function getAliasText(alias) {
  400:   if (typeof alias === "string") {
  401:     return alias.trim();
  402:   }
  403: 
  404:   return getText(alias?.name || alias?.value || alias?.label);
  405: }
  406: 
  407: function validateAliases(collector, skill) {
  408:   const aliases = getArray(skill.aliases);
  409: 
  410:   if (aliases.length > SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumAliases) {
  411:     collector.addError(
  412:       "aliases",
  413:       `Add no more than ${SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumAliases} aliases.`,
  414:       "too_many_items",
  415:     );
  416:   }
  417: 
  418:   const skillComparisonName = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(skill.name);
  419: 
  420:   const seenAliases = new Map();
  421: 
  422:   aliases.forEach((alias, index) => {
  423:     const aliasText = getAliasText(alias);
  424: 
  425:     if (!aliasText) {
  426:       collector.addError(
  427:         `aliases.${index}`,
  428:         "Skill alias cannot be empty.",
  429:         "required",
  430:       );
  431: 
  432:       return;
  433:     }
  434: 
  435:     validateTextLength(
  436:       collector,
  437:       `aliases.${index}`,
  438:       aliasText,
  439:       SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.alias,
  440:       "Skill alias",
  441:     );
  442: 
  443:     const comparisonAlias = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(aliasText);
  444: 
  445:     if (comparisonAlias === skillComparisonName) {
  446:       collector.addError(
  447:         `aliases.${index}`,
  448:         "An alias cannot be the same as the primary skill name.",
  449:         "alias_matches_name",
  450:       );
  451:     }
  452: 
  453:     if (seenAliases.has(comparisonAlias)) {
  454:       collector.addError(
  455:         `aliases.${index}`,
  456:         "This skill alias has already been added.",
  457:         "duplicate_alias",
  458:       );
  459: 
  460:       return;
  461:     }
  462: 
  463:     seenAliases.set(comparisonAlias, index);
  464:   });
  465: }
  466: 
  467: /*
  468:  * =========================================
  469:  * Existing Library Helpers
  470:  * =========================================
  471:  */
  472: 
  473: function getSkillNames(skill) {
  474:   const skillObject = getObject(skill);
  475: 
  476:   return [
  477:     getText(skillObject.name),
  478: 
  479:     ...getArray(skillObject.aliases).map(getAliasText),
  480:   ]
  481:     .map(normalizeSkillNameForComparison)
  482:     .filter(Boolean);
  483: }
  484: 
  485: /*
  486:  * =========================================
  487:  * Existing Library Conflicts
  488:  * =========================================
  489:  */
  490: 
  491: function validateLibraryConflicts(
  492:   collector,
  493:   skill,
  494:   existingSkills,
  495:   excludeSkillId,
  496: ) {
  497:   const proposedName = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(skill.name);
  498: 
  499:   if (!proposedName) {
  500:     return;
  501:   }
  502: 
  503:   const proposedAliases = getArray(skill.aliases)
  504:     .map(getAliasText)
  505:     .map(normalizeSkillNameForComparison)
  506:     .filter(Boolean);
  507: 
  508:   getArray(existingSkills).forEach((existingSkill) => {
  509:     if (
  510:       existingSkill?.id === excludeSkillId ||
  511:       existingSkill?.id === skill.id
  512:     ) {
  513:       return;
  514:     }
  515: 
  516:     const existingNames = getSkillNames(existingSkill);
  517: 
  518:     if (existingNames.includes(proposedName)) {
  519:       collector.addConflict(
  520:         "name",
  521:         `"${skill.name}" already exists in your Skill Library or matches an existing alias.`,
  522:         existingSkill,
  523:         "duplicate_library_skill",
  524:       );
  525: 
  526:       return;
  527:     }
  528: 
  529:     proposedAliases.forEach((proposedAlias, aliasIndex) => {
  530:       if (existingNames.includes(proposedAlias)) {
  531:         collector.addConflict(
  532:           `aliases.${aliasIndex}`,
  533:           `This alias is already used by "${existingSkill.name}".`,
  534:           existingSkill,
  535:           "duplicate_library_alias",
  536:         );
  537:       }
  538:     });
  539:   });
  540: }
  541: 
  542: /*
  543:  * =========================================
  544:  * Meaningful Information
  545:  * =========================================
  546:  */
  547: 
  548: function validateMeaningfulInformation(collector, skill) {
  549:   const name = getText(skill.name);
  550:   const description = getText(skill.description);
  551: 
  552:   if (name && name.split(/\s+/).length === 1 && name.length <= 2) {
  553:     collector.addWarning(
  554:       "name",
  555:       "Review this short skill name and consider using its complete professional name.",
  556:       "short_skill_name",
  557:     );
  558:   }
  559: 
  560:   if (!description) {
  561:     collector.addWarning(
  562:       "description",
  563:       "Consider adding a short description explaining how you use this skill.",
  564:       "missing_description",
  565:     );
  566:   }
  567: 
  568:   if (skill.source === "ai-suggested" && !getText(skill.sourceContext)) {
  569:     collector.addWarning(
  570:       "sourceContext",
  571:       "The origin of this AI-suggested skill was not recorded.",
  572:       "missing_source_context",
  573:     );
  574:   }
  575: }
  576: 
  577: /*
  578:  * =========================================
  579:  * Field Error Map
  580:  * =========================================
  581:  */
  582: 
  583: export function createSkillFieldErrorMap(errors = []) {
  584:   return errors.reduce((fieldErrors, error) => {
  585:     if (!fieldErrors[error.field]) {
  586:       fieldErrors[error.field] = error.message;
  587:     }
  588: 
  589:     return fieldErrors;
  590:   }, {});
  591: }
  592: 
  593: /*
  594:  * =========================================
  595:  * Main Skill Validation
  596:  * =========================================
  597:  */
  598: 
  599: export function validateSkill(
  600:   skillValue,
  601:   { existingSkills = [], excludeSkillId = "" } = {},
  602: ) {
  603:   const skill = getObject(skillValue);
  604: 
  605:   const collector = createValidationCollector();
  606: 
  607:   validateSkillName(collector, skill);
  608: 
  609:   validateConfiguredOptions(collector, skill);
  610: 
  611:   validateSupportingText(collector, skill);
  612: 
  613:   validateProficiency(collector, skill);
  614: 
  615:   validateLanguageRules(collector, skill);
  616: 
  617:   validateAliases(collector, skill);
  618: 
  619:   validateLibraryConflicts(collector, skill, existingSkills, excludeSkillId);
  620: 
  621:   validateMeaningfulInformation(collector, skill);
  622: 
  623:   return {
  624:     isValid: collector.errors.length === 0,
  625: 
  626:     errors: collector.errors,
  627:     warnings: collector.warnings,
  628:     conflicts: collector.conflicts,
  629: 
  630:     fieldErrors: createSkillFieldErrorMap(collector.errors),
  631:   };
  632: }
  633: 
  634: /*
  635:  * =========================================
  636:  * Skill Name Availability
  637:  * =========================================
  638:  */
  639: 
  640: export function validateSkillNameAvailability(
  641:   name,
  642:   existingSkills = [],
  643:   excludeSkillId = "",
  644: ) {
  645:   const normalizedName = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(name);
  646: 
  647:   if (!normalizedName) {
  648:     return {
  649:       isAvailable: false,
  650:       matchingSkill: null,
  651:       message: "Enter a skill name.",
  652:     };
  653:   }
  654: 
  655:   const matchingSkill =
  656:     getArray(existingSkills).find((skill) => {
  657:       if (skill?.id === excludeSkillId) {
  658:         return false;
  659:       }
  660: 
  661:       return getSkillNames(skill).includes(normalizedName);
  662:     }) || null;
  663: 
  664:   if (matchingSkill) {
  665:     return {
  666:       isAvailable: false,
  667:       matchingSkill,
  668: 
  669:       message: `"${name.trim()}" already exists as "${matchingSkill.name}".`,
  670:     };
  671:   }
  672: 
  673:   return {
  674:     isAvailable: true,
  675:     matchingSkill: null,
  676:     message: "This skill name is available.",
  677:   };
  678: }
  679: 
  680: /*
  681:  * =========================================
  682:  * Assert Valid Skill
  683:  * =========================================
  684:  */
  685: 
  686: export function assertValidSkill(skill, options = {}) {
  687:   const validationResult = validateSkill(skill, options);
  688: 
  689:   if (validationResult.isValid) {
  690:     return validationResult;
  691:   }
  692: 
  693:   const error = new Error(
  694:     "The skill contains invalid or incomplete information.",
  695:   );
  696: 
  697:   error.name = "SkillValidationError";
  698:   error.code = "SKILL_VALIDATION_FAILED";
  699:   error.status = 400;
  700: 
  701:   error.publicMessage =
  702:     "Correct the highlighted skill information before saving.";
  703: 
  704:   error.validation = validationResult;
  705:   error.errors = validationResult.errors;
  706:   error.warnings = validationResult.warnings;
  707:   error.conflicts = validationResult.conflicts;
  708:   error.fieldErrors = validationResult.fieldErrors;
  709: 
  710:   throw error;
  711: }
  712: 
  713: /*
  714:  * =========================================
  715:  * Validation Summary
  716:  * =========================================
  717:  */
  718: 
  719: export function getSkillValidationSummary(skill, options = {}) {
  720:   const validationResult = validateSkill(skill, options);
  721: 
  722:   if (!validationResult.isValid) {
  723:     return `${validationResult.errors.length} ${
  724:       validationResult.errors.length === 1 ? "problem must" : "problems must"
  725:     } be corrected before saving.`;
  726:   }
  727: 
  728:   if (validationResult.warnings.length > 0) {
  729:     return `Ready to save with ${validationResult.warnings.length} ${
  730:       validationResult.warnings.length === 1 ? "suggestion" : "suggestions"
  731:     } for improvement.`;
  732:   }
  733: 
  734:   return "This skill is complete and ready to save.";
  735: }

  244:             Dismiss
  245:           </button>
  246:         </section>
  247:       )}
  248: 
  249:       {saveStatus === "saving" && (
  250:         <section
  251:           className="skills-page-message skills-page-message--saving"
  252:           role="status"
  253:           aria-live="polite"
  254:         >
  255:           <span className="skills-page-message-loader" aria-hidden="true" />
  256: 
  257:           <div>
  258:             <strong>Saving skill</strong>
  259: 
  260:             <p>Your Skill Library is being updated.</p>
  261:           </div>
  262:         </section>
  263:       )}
  264: 
  265:       {isFormOpen && (
  266:         <section className="skills-page-form">
  267:           <header className="skills-page-form-header">
  268:             <div>
  269:               <span>Skill Editor</span>
  270: 
  271:               <h2>
  272:                 {editingSkill
  273:                   ? `Edit ${editingSkill.name}`
  274:                   : "Add Professional Skill"}
  275:               </h2>
  276: 
  277:               <p>
  278:                 Create a reusable Skill Library record for Experiences,
  279:                 Education, Training, Resumes, and Portfolios.
  280:               </p>
  281:             </div>
  282: 
  283:             <button
  284:               type="button"
  285:               className="skills-page-form-close"
  286:               onClick={handleCloseForm}
  287:               aria-label="Close skill form"
  288:             >
  289:               ×
  290:             </button>
  291:           </header>
  292: 
  293:           <SkillForm
  294:             initialSkill={editingSkill}
  295:             existingSkills={skills}
  296:             isSaving={saveStatus === "saving"}
  297:             onSubmit={handleSaveSkill}
  298:             onCancel={handleCloseForm}
  299:           />
  300:         </section>
  301:       )}
  302: 
  303:       <SkillsLibrary
  304:         skills={selectedSkills}
  305:         showArchived={showArchived}
  306:         isLoading={isLoading}
  307:         isUsageLoading={isUsageLoading}
  308:         loadStatus={loadStatus}
  309:         operation={operation}
  310:         usageBySkillId={usageBySkillId}
  311:         conflictError={error?.code === "SKILL_IN_USE" ? error : null}
  312:         onDismissConflict={clearError}
  313:         onAddSkill={handleAddSkill}
  314:         onRetry={handleRetryLoading}
  315:         onEdit={handleEditSkill}
  316:         onArchive={handleArchiveSkill}
  317:         onRestore={handleRestoreSkill}
  318:         onDelete={handleDeleteSkill}
  319:       />
  320:     </main>
  321:   );
  322: }
  323: 
  324: export default Skills;

   29: function createFormData(initialSkill) {
   30:   if (initialSkill) {
   31:     return normalizeSkill(initialSkill);
   32:   }
   33: 
   34:   return {
   35:     ...createEmptySkill(),
   36:     source: "manual",

   41: /*
   42:  * =========================================
   43:  * Skill Form

   45:  */
   46: 
   47: function SkillForm({
   48:   initialSkill = null,
   49:   existingSkills = [],
   50:   isSaving = false,
   51:   onSubmit,
   52:   onCancel,
   53: }) {
   54:   const formId = useId();
   55: 
   56:   const [formData, setFormData] = useState(() => createFormData(initialSkill));
   57: 
   58:   const [aliasDraft, setAliasDraft] = useState("");
   59: 
   60:   const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = useState({});
   61: 
   62:   const [warnings, setWarnings] = useState([]);
   63: 
   64:   const [submissionError, setSubmissionError] = useState("");
   65: 
   66:   const [aliasError, setAliasError] = useState("");
   67: 
   68:   const isEditing = Boolean(initialSkill?.id);
   69: 
   70:   /*
   71:    * =========================================
   72:    * Synchronize Editing Record
   73:    * =========================================
   74:    */
   75: 
   76:   useEffect(() => {
   77:     setFormData(createFormData(initialSkill));
   78:     setAliasDraft("");
   79:     setFieldErrors({});
   80:     setWarnings([]);
   81:     setSubmissionError("");
   82:     setAliasError("");
   83:   }, [initialSkill]);
   84: 
   85:   /*
   86:    * =========================================
   87:    * Error Management
   88:    * =========================================
   89:    */
   90: 
   91:   const clearFieldError = (fieldName) => {
   92:     setFieldErrors((currentErrors) => {
   93:       const nextErrors = {};
   94: 
   95:       Object.entries(currentErrors).forEach(([errorField, message]) => {
   96:         if (
   97:           errorField !== fieldName &&
   98:           !errorField.startsWith(`${fieldName}.`)
   99:         ) {
  100:           nextErrors[errorField] = message;
  101:         }
  102:       });
  103: 
  104:       return nextErrors;
  105:     });
  106: 
  107:     setSubmissionError("");
  108:   };
  109: 
  110:   /*
  111:    * =========================================
  112:    * Top-Level Update
  113:    * =========================================
  114:    */
  115: 
  116:   const updateField = (fieldName, value) => {
  117:     setFormData((currentData) => ({
  118:       ...currentData,
  119:       [fieldName]: value,
  120:     }));
  121: 
  122:     clearFieldError(fieldName);
  123:   };
  124: 
  125:   /*
  126:    * =========================================
  127:    * Nested Update
  128:    * =========================================
  129:    */
  130: 
  131:   const updateNestedField = (sectionName, fieldName, value) => {
  132:     setFormData((currentData) => ({
  133:       ...currentData,
  134: 
  135:       [sectionName]: {
  136:         ...currentData[sectionName],
  137:         [fieldName]: value,
  138:       },
  139:     }));
  140: 
  141:     clearFieldError(`${sectionName}.${fieldName}`);
  142:   };
  143: 
  144:   /*
  145:    * =========================================
  146:    * Category and Type Consistency
  147:    * =========================================
  148:    */
  149: 
  150:   const handleCategoryChange = (event) => {
  151:     const category = event.target.value;
  152: 
  153:     setFormData((currentData) => ({
  154:       ...currentData,
  155:       category,
  156: 
  157:       /*
  158:        * A skill in the Languages category must
  159:        * use the Language Skill type.
  160:        */
  161: 
  162:       type:
  163:         category === "language"
  164:           ? "language"
  165:           : currentData.type === "language"
  166:             ? "professional"
  167:             : currentData.type,
  168: 
  169:       language: {
  170:         ...currentData.language,
  171: 
  172:         proficiency:
  173:           category === "language" ? currentData.language.proficiency : "",
  174:       },
  175:     }));
  176: 
  177:     clearFieldError("category");
  178:     clearFieldError("type");
  179:     clearFieldError("language.proficiency");
  180:   };
  181: 
  182:   const handleTypeChange = (event) => {
  183:     const type = event.target.value;
  184: 
  185:     setFormData((currentData) => ({
  186:       ...currentData,
  187:       type,
  188: 
  189:       category:
  190:         type === "language"
  191:           ? "language"
  192:           : currentData.category === "language"
  193:             ? "other"
  194:             : currentData.category,
  195: 
  196:       language: {
  197:         ...currentData.language,
  198: 
  199:         proficiency:
  200:           type === "language" ? currentData.language.proficiency : "",
  201:       },
  202:     }));
  203: 
  204:     clearFieldError("type");
  205:     clearFieldError("category");
  206:     clearFieldError("language.proficiency");
  207:   };
  208: 
  209:   /*
  210:    * =========================================
  211:    * Alias Management
  212:    * =========================================
  213:    */
  214: 
  215:   const handleAddAlias = () => {
  216:     const alias = aliasDraft.trim();
  217: 
  218:     if (!alias) {
  219:       setAliasError("Enter an alias before adding it.");
  220: 
  221:       return;
  222:     }
  223: 
  224:     if (alias.length > SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.alias) {
  225:       setAliasError(
  226:         `An alias cannot exceed ${SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.alias} characters.`,
  227:       );
  228: 
  229:       return;
  230:     }
  231: 
  232:     if (formData.aliases.length >= SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumAliases) {
  233:       setAliasError(
  234:         `Add no more than ${SKILL_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumAliases} aliases.`,
  235:       );
  236: 
  237:       return;
  238:     }
  239: 
  240:     const normalizedAlias = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(alias);
  241: 
  242:     const normalizedSkillName = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(formData.name);
  243: 
  244:     if (normalizedSkillName && normalizedAlias === normalizedSkillName) {
  245:       setAliasError("An alias cannot be the same as the primary skill name.");
  246: 
  247:       return;
  248:     }
  249: 
  250:     const isDuplicate = formData.aliases.some(
  251:       (existingAlias) =>
  252:         normalizeSkillNameForComparison(existingAlias) === normalizedAlias,
  253:     );
  254: 
  255:     if (isDuplicate) {
  256:       setAliasError("This alias has already been added.");
  257: 
  258:       return;
  259:     }
  260: 
  261:     setFormData((currentData) => ({
  262:       ...currentData,
  263:       aliases: [...currentData.aliases, alias],
  264:     }));
  265: 
  266:     setAliasDraft("");
  267:     setAliasError("");
  268:     clearFieldError("aliases");
  269:   };
  270:

  273:       return;
  274:     }
  275: 
  276:     event.preventDefault();
  277: 
  278:     handleAddAlias();
  279:   };
  280: 
  281:   const handleRemoveAlias = (aliasIndex) => {

  283:       ...currentData,
  284: 
  285:       aliases: currentData.aliases.filter((_, index) => index !== aliasIndex),
  286:     }));
  287: 
  288:     setAliasError("");
  289:     clearFieldError("aliases");
  290:   };
  291:

  299:     event.preventDefault();
  300: 
  301:     setSubmissionError("");
  302:     setAliasError("");

  315:           validationResult.errors.length === 1

  329:

  552:                 </div>

  833:     </form>

  839:  * Form Field
  840:  * =========================================

  842: 
  843: function FormField({
  844:   id,
  845:   label,
  846:   required = false,
  847:   error = "",
  848:   help = "",
  849:   count = "",
  850:   className = "",
  851:   children,
  852: }) {
  853:   return (
  854:     <div
  855:       className={`skill-form-field ${className} ${
  856:         error ? "skill-form-field--error" : ""
  857:       }`}
  858:     >
  859:       <div className="skill-form-label-row">
  860:         <label htmlFor={id}>
  861:           {label}
  862: 
  863:           {required && <span aria-hidden="true">*</span>}
  864:         </label>
  865: 
  866:         {count && <small>{count}</small>}
  867:       </div>
  868: 
  869:       {children}
  870: 
  871:       {help && !error && <small className="skill-form-help">{help}</small>}
  872: 
  873:       {error && (
  874:         <p className="skill-form-field-error" role="alert">
  875:           {error}
  876:         </p>
  877:       )}
  878:     </div>
  879:   );
  880: }
  881: 
  882: export default SkillForm;

    2:

    4:   createEmptySkillUsage,

   13: } from "../../../../services/Skill/skillUtils.js";
   14:

   16: 
   17: function SkillItem({
   18:   skill,
   19:   usage,
   20:   isWorking = false,
   21:   isUsageLoading = false,
   22:   conflictError = null,
   23:   onDismissConflict,
   24:   onEdit,
   25:   onArchive,
   26:   onRestore,
   27:   onDelete,
   28: }) {
   29:   const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);
   30: 
   31:   if (!skill) {
   32:     return null;
   33:   }
   34: 
   35:   const resolvedUsage = usage || createEmptySkillUsage(skill.id);
   36: 
   37:   const isInUse = isSkillInUse(resolvedUsage);
   38: 
   39:   const usageValue = (value) => (isUsageLoading ? "—" : value);
   40: 
   41:   const completeness = getSkillCompleteness(skill);
   42: 
   43:   const category = getSkillCategoryDisplay(skill);
   44: 
   45:   const type = getSkillTypeDisplay(skill);
   46: 
   47:   const proficiency = getSkillProficiencyDisplay(skill);
   48: 
   49:   const source = getSkillSourceDisplay(skill);
   50: 
   51:   const lastUsedDate = formatSkillLastUsedDate(skill.proficiency?.lastUsedDate);
   52: 
   53:   const yearsOfExperience = skill.proficiency?.yearsOfExperience;
   54: 
   55:   const hasYearsOfExperience =
   56:     yearsOfExperience !== null &&
   57:     yearsOfExperience !== undefined &&
   58:     yearsOfExperience !== "";
   59: 
   60:   const aliases = Array.isArray(skill.aliases)
   61:     ? skill.aliases.filter(Boolean)
   62:     : [];
   63: 
   64:   const isArchived = skill.status === "archived";
   65: 
   66:   const hasAdditionalDetails = Boolean(
   67:     skill.description ||
   68:     aliases.length > 0 ||
   69:     hasYearsOfExperience ||
   70:     lastUsedDate ||
   71:     skill.notes ||
   72:     skill.sourceContext ||
   73:     resolvedUsage.experienceCount > 0 ||
   74:     resolvedUsage.educationCount > 0 ||
   75:     resolvedUsage.trainingCount > 0,
   76:   );
   77: 
   78:   return (
   79:     <article
   80:       className={`skill-item ${isExpanded ? "skill-item--expanded" : ""} ${
   81:         isArchived ? "skill-item--archived" : ""
   82:       }`}
   83:     >
   84:       <header className="skill-item-summary">

  245:             onClick={onDismissConflict}
  246:           >
  247:             Dismiss
  248:           </button>
  249:         </section>

  254:           <span aria-hidden="true" />

  258:       )}
  259: 
  260:       <section
  261:         className="skill-item-usage"

  263:         aria-busy={isUsageLoading}

  267:             <span>Library Usage</span>

  278:               isInUse ? "skill-item-usage-total--used" : ""

  435:             )}
  436:

  438:               label="Record Status"

  440:             />
  441:           </div>

  450:                 ))}
  451:               </div>
  452:             </section>
  453:           )}
  454: 
  455:           {skill.description && (

  461:           )}
  462:

  466:                 <span>Private Notes</span>

  469:               </header>
  470: 
  471:               <p>{skill.notes}</p>
  472:             </section>
  473:           )}
  474: 
  475:           <section className="skill-item-completeness">
  476:             <header>
  477:               <div>
  478:                 <span>Record Quality</span>
  479: 
  480:                 <strong>{completeness.label}</strong>
  481:               </div>
  482: 
  483:               <strong>{completeness.percentage}%</strong>
  484:             </header>
  485: 
  486:             <div className="skill-item-completeness-progress">
  487:               <span
  488:                 style={{
  489:                   width: `${completeness.percentage}%`,
  490:                 }}
  491:               />
  492:             </div>
  493: 
  494:             {completeness.missingChecks.length > 0 && (
  495:               <div className="skill-item-missing">
  496:                 <span>Suggested improvements</span>
  497: 
  498:                 <ul>
  499:                   {completeness.missingChecks.map((check) => (
  500:                     <li key={check.id}>Add {check.label.toLowerCase()}</li>
  501:                   ))}
  502:                 </ul>
  503:               </div>
  504:             )}
  505:           </section>
  506: 
  507:           <footer className="skill-item-details-footer">
  508:             <button
  509:               type="button"
  510:               onClick={() => setIsExpanded(false)}
  511:               disabled={isWorking}
  512:             >
  513:               Hide Details
  514:             </button>
  515: 
  516:             {!isArchived && (
  517:               <button
  518:                 type="button"
  519:                 className="skill-item-details-edit"
  520:                 onClick={() => onEdit?.(skill)}
  521:                 disabled={isWorking}
  522:               >
  523:                 Edit This Skill
  524:               </button>
  525:             )}
  526:           </footer>
  527:         </div>
  528:       )}
  529:     </article>
  530:   );
  531: }
  532: 
  533: function Detail({ label, value }) {
  534:   if (value === undefined || value === null || value === "") {
  535:     return null;
  536:   }
  537: 
  538:   return (
  539:     <div className="skill-item-detail">
  540:       <span>{label}</span>
  541:       <strong>{value}</strong>
  542:     </div>
  543:   );
  544: }
  545: 
  546: export default SkillItem;

   21:  * Skill Selector

   24: 
   25: function SkillSelector({
   26:   skills = [],
   27:   selectedSkillIds = [],
   28:   maximumSelections = 50,
   29:   disabled = false,
   30: 
   31:   allowCreate = true,
   32:   defaultNewSkillCategory = "other",
   33:   defaultNewSkillType = "professional",
   34: 
   35:   title = "Select Skills",
   36:   description = "Search your central Skill Library and select reusable professional skills.",
   37:   emptyMessage = "No active skills are available.",
   38: 
   39:   onSelect,
   40:   onRemove,
   41:   onCreateSkill,
   42: }) {
   43:   const selectorId = useId();
   44: 
   45:   const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
   46: 
   47:   const [category, setCategory] = useState("all");
   48: 
   49:   const [type, setType] = useState("all");
   50: 
   51:   const [newSkillCategory, setNewSkillCategory] = useState(
   52:     defaultNewSkillCategory,
   53:   );
   54: 
   55:   const [newSkillType, setNewSkillType] = useState(defaultNewSkillType);
   56: 
   57:   const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");
   58: 
   59:   const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
   60: 
   61:   /*
   62:    * =========================================
   63:    * Normalized Input
   64:    * =========================================
   65:    */
   66: 
   67:   const activeSkills = useMemo(() => getActiveSkills(skills), [skills]);
   68: 
   69:   const selectedIds = useMemo(
   70:     () =>
   71:       new Set(
   72:         (selectedSkillIds || [])
   73:           .map((value) =>
   74:             typeof value === "string" ? value : value?.skillId || value?.id,
   75:           )
   76:           .filter(Boolean),
   77:       ),
   78:     [selectedSkillIds],
   79:   );
   80: 
   81:   const selectedSkills = useMemo(
   82:     () => activeSkills.filter((skill) => selectedIds.has(skill.id)),
   83:     [activeSkills, selectedIds],
   84:   );
   85: 
   86:   /*
   87:    * =========================================
   88:    * Search Results
   89:    * =========================================
   90:    */
   91: 
   92:   const searchResults = useMemo(
   93:     () =>
   94:       prepareSkillCollection({
   95:         skills: activeSkills,
   96:         query,
   97:         filters: {
   98:           category,
   99:           type,
  100:           proficiency: "all",
  101:           status: "active",
  102:         },
  103:         sortOption: "name-ascending",
  104:       }),
  105:     [activeSkills, query, category, type],
  106:   );
  107: 
  108:   const availableResults = useMemo(
  109:     () => searchResults.filter((skill) => !selectedIds.has(skill.id)),
  110:     [searchResults, selectedIds],
  111:   );
  112: 
  113:   const normalizedQuery = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(query);
  114: 
  115:   const exactLibraryMatch = useMemo(() => {
  116:     if (!normalizedQuery) {
  117:       return null;
  118:     }
  119: 
  120:     return (
  121:       activeSkills.find((skill) => {
  122:         if (normalizeSkillNameForComparison(skill.name) === normalizedQuery) {
  123:           return true;
  124:         }
  125: 
  126:         return (skill.aliases || []).some(
  127:           (alias) => normalizeSkillNameForComparison(alias) === normalizedQuery,
  128:         );
  129:       }) || null
  130:     );
  131:   }, [activeSkills, normalizedQuery]);
  132: 
  133:   const hasReachedMaximum = selectedIds.size >= maximumSelections;
  134: 
  135:   const canCreateSkill =
  136:     allowCreate &&
  137:     Boolean(query.trim()) &&
  138:     !exactLibraryMatch &&
  139:     !hasReachedMaximum &&
  140:     typeof onCreateSkill === "function";
  141: 
  142:   const isWorking = disabled || status === "loading";
  143: 
  144:   /*
  145:    * =========================================
  146:    * Select Existing Skill
  147:    * =========================================
  148:    */
  149: 
  150:   const handleSelect = async (skill) => {
  151:     if (isWorking || hasReachedMaximum || selectedIds.has(skill.id)) {
  152:       return;
  153:     }
  154: 
  155:     try {
  156:       setStatus("loading");
  157:       setMessage("");
  158: 
  159:       await onSelect?.(skill);
  160: 
  161:       setQuery("");
  162:       setStatus("success");
  163:       setMessage(`"${skill.name}" was selected.`);
  164:     } catch (error) {
  165:       setStatus("error");
  166: 
  167:       setMessage(
  168:         error?.publicMessage ||
  169:           error?.message ||
  170:           "The skill could not be selected.",
  171:       );
  172:     }
  173:   };
  174: 
  175:   /*
  176:    * =========================================
  177:    * Remove Selected Skill
  178:    * =========================================
  179:    */
  180: 
  181:   const handleRemove = async (skill) => {
  182:     if (isWorking) {
  183:       return;
  184:     }
  185: 
  186:     try {
  187:       setStatus("loading");
  188:       setMessage("");
  189: 
  190:       await onRemove?.(skill);
  191: 
  192:       setStatus("success");
  193:       setMessage(
  194:         `"${skill.name}" was removed from this selection. The Skill Library record was not deleted.`,
  195:       );
  196:     } catch (error) {
  197:       setStatus("error");
  198: 
  199:       setMessage(
  200:         error?.publicMessage ||
  201:           error?.message ||
  202:           "The skill could not be removed.",
  203:       );
  204:     }
  205:   };
  206: 
  207:   /*
  208:    * =========================================
  209:    * Create and Select Skill
  210:    * =========================================
  211:    */
  212: 
  213:   const handleCreateSkill = async () => {
  214:     const name = query.trim();
  215: 
  216:     if (!name || !canCreateSkill || isWorking) {
  217:       return;
  218:     }
  219: 
  220:     try {
  221:       setStatus("loading");
  222:       setMessage("");
  223: 
  224:       const result = await onCreateSkill({
  225:         name,
  226:         category: newSkillCategory,
  227:         type: newSkillType,
  228:         source: "manual",
  229:       });
  230: 
  231:       const createdSkill = result?.skill || result;
  232: 
  233:       if (!createdSkill?.id) {
  234:         throw new Error(
  235:           "The new Skill Library record could not be identified.",
  236:         );
  237:       }
  238: 
  239:       /*
  240:        * Some consumers may create and attach the
  241:        * relationship inside onCreateSkill.
  242:        *
  243:        * Set attached=true in the returned object to
  244:        * prevent the selector from calling onSelect
  245:        * a second time.
  246:        */
  247: 
  248:       if (result?.attached !== true) {
  249:         await onSelect?.(createdSkill);
  250:       }
  251: 
  252:       setQuery("");
  253:       setNewSkillCategory(defaultNewSkillCategory);
  254:       setNewSkillType(defaultNewSkillType);
  255: 
  256:       setStatus("success");
  257: 
  258:       setMessage(
  259:         result?.created === false
  260:           ? `"${createdSkill.name}" already existed and was selected.`
  261:           : `"${createdSkill.name}" was added to the Skill Library and selected.`,
  262:       );
  263:     } catch (error) {
  264:       setStatus("error");
  265: 
  266:       setMessage(
  267:         error?.publicMessage ||
  268:           error?.message ||
  269:           "The skill could not be created.",
  270:       );
  271:     }
  272:   };
  273: 
  274:   /*
  275:    * =========================================
  276:    * Clear Controls
  277:    * =========================================
  278:    */
  279: 
  280:   const handleClearControls = () => {
  281:     setQuery("");
  282:     setCategory("all");
  283:     setType("all");
  284:     setMessage("");
  285:     setStatus("idle");
  286:   };
  287: 
  288:   const hasFilters = Boolean(query) || category !== "all" || type !== "all";
  289: 
  290:   return (
  291:     <section className="skill-selector" aria-labelledby={`${selectorId}-title`}>
  292:       <header className="skill-selector-header">
  293:         <div>
  294:           <span>Shared Skill Library</span>
  295: 
  296:           <h3 id={`${selectorId}-title`}>{title}</h3>
  297: 
  298:           <p>{description}</p>
  299:         </div>
  300: 
  301:         <span className="skill-selector-count">
  302:           {selectedIds.size}/{maximumSelections}
  303:         </span>
  304:       </header>
  305: 
  306:       {/*
  307:        * =========================================
  308:        * Selected Skills
  309:        * =========================================
  310:        */}
  311: 
  312:       {selectedSkills.length > 0 && (
  313:         <section className="skill-selector-selected">
  314:           <header>
  315:             <span>Currently Selected</span>
  316: 
  317:             <strong>{selectedSkills.length}</strong>
  318:           </header>
  319: 
  320:           <div>
  321:             {selectedSkills.map((skill) => (
  322:               <article key={skill.id}>
  323:                 <div>
  324:                   <strong>{skill.name}</strong>
  325: 
  326:                   <small>{getSkillCategoryLabel(skill.category)}</small>
  327:                 </div>
  328: 
  329:                 {onRemove && (
  330:                   <button
  331:                     type="button"
  332:                     onClick={() => handleRemove(skill)}
  333:                     disabled={isWorking}
  334:                     aria-label={`Remove ${skill.name} from this selection`}
  335:                   >
  336:                     ×
  337:                   </button>
  338:                 )}
  339:               </article>
  340:             ))}
  341:           </div>
  342:         </section>
  343:       )}
  344: 
  345:       {/*
  346:        * =========================================
  347:        * Search
  348:        * =========================================
  349:        */}
  350: 
  351:       <div className="skill-selector-search">
  352:         <label htmlFor={`${selectorId}-search`}>Search or Add a Skill</label>
  353: 
  354:         <div>
  355:           <span aria-hidden="true">⌕</span>
  356: 
  357:           <input
  358:             id={`${selectorId}-search`}
  359:             type="search"
  360:             value={query}
  361:             onChange={(event) => {
  362:               setQuery(event.target.value);
  363:               setMessage("");
  364:               setStatus("idle");
  365:             }}
  366:             disabled={isWorking}
  367:             placeholder="Example: Python, Active Directory, Azure"
  368:             autoComplete="off"
  369:           />
  370: 
  371:           {query && (
  372:             <button
  373:               type="button"
  374:               onClick={() => setQuery("")}
  375:               disabled={isWorking}
  376:               aria-label="Clear skill search"
  377:             >
  378:               ×
  379:             </button>
  380:           )}
  381:         </div>
  382:       </div>
  383: 
  384:       {/*
  385:        * =========================================
  386:        * Filters
  387:        * =========================================
  388:        */}
  389: 
  390:       <div className="skill-selector-filters">
  391:         <div>
  392:           <label htmlFor={`${selectorId}-category`}>Category</label>
  393: 
  394:           <select
  395:             id={`${selectorId}-category`}
  396:             value={category}
  397:             onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value)}
  398:             disabled={isWorking}
  399:           >
  400:             <option value="all">All Categories</option>
  401: 
  402:             {SKILL_CATEGORY_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
  403:               <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
  404:                 {option.label}
  405:               </option>
  406:             ))}
  407:           </select>
  408:         </div>
  409: 
  410:         <div>
  411:           <label htmlFor={`${selectorId}-type`}>Skill Type</label>
  412: 
  413:           <select
  414:             id={`${selectorId}-type`}
  415:             value={type}
  416:             onChange={(event) => setType(event.target.value)}
  417:             disabled={isWorking}
  418:           >
  419:             <option value="all">All Types</option>
  420: 
  421:             {SKILL_TYPE_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
  422:               <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
  423:                 {option.label}
  424:               </option>
  425:             ))}
  426:           </select>
  427:         </div>
  428: 
  429:         {hasFilters && (
  430:           <button
  431:             type="button"
  432:             onClick={handleClearControls}
  433:             disabled={isWorking}
  434:           >
  435:             Clear
  436:           </button>
  437:         )}
  438:       </div>
  439: 
  440:       {/*
  441:        * =========================================
  442:        * Status
  443:        * =========================================
  444:        */}
  445: 
  446:       {message && (
  447:         <div
  448:           className={`skill-selector-message skill-selector-message--${status}`}
  449:           role={status === "error" ? "alert" : "status"}
  450:         >
  451:           <span>{message}</span>
  452: 
  453:           <button
  454:             type="button"
  455:             onClick={() => {
  456:               setMessage("");
  457:               setStatus("idle");
  458:             }}
  459:             aria-label="Dismiss selector message"
  460:           >
  461:             ×
  462:           </button>
  463:         </div>
  464:       )}
  465: 
  466:       {hasReachedMaximum && (
  467:         <div className="skill-selector-limit" role="status">
  468:           The maximum of {maximumSelections} skills has been selected.
  469:         </div>
  470:       )}
  471: 
  472:       {/*
  473:        * =========================================
  474:        * Search Results
  475:        * =========================================
  476:        */}
  477: 
  478:       <section className="skill-selector-results">
  479:         <header>
  480:           <div>
  481:             <span>Available Skills</span>
  482: 
  483:             <h4>Skill Library Results</h4>
  484:           </div>
  485: 
  486:           <span>
  487:             {availableResults.length}{" "}
  488:             {availableResults.length === 1 ? "result" : "results"}
  489:           </span>
  490:         </header>
  491: 
  492:         {activeSkills.length === 0 ? (
  493:           <div className="skill-selector-empty">
  494:             <span aria-hidden="true">✦</span>
  495: 
  496:             <h5>No active skills available</h5>
  497: 
  498:             <p>{emptyMessage}</p>
  499:           </div>
  500:         ) : availableResults.length > 0 ? (
  501:           <div className="skill-selector-result-list">
  502:             {availableResults.map((skill) => (
  503:               <article key={skill.id}>
  504:                 <div className="skill-selector-result-icon">
  505:                   <span aria-hidden="true">✦</span>
  506:                 </div>

  508:                 <div className="skill-selector-result-content">

  510:                     <span>{getSkillCategoryLabel(skill.category)}</span>
  511: 
  512:                     <span>{getSkillTypeLabel(skill.type)}</span>
  513:                   </div>
  514: 
  515:                   <strong>{skill.name}</strong>
  516: 
  517:                   {skill.description && <p>{skill.description}</p>}
  518:                 </div>
  519: 
  520:                 <button
  521:                   type="button"
  522:                   onClick={() => handleSelect(skill)}
  523:                   disabled={isWorking || hasReachedMaximum}
  524:                 >
  525:                   Select
  526:                 </button>
  527:               </article>
  528:             ))}
  529:           </div>
  530:         ) : (
  531:           <div className="skill-selector-empty">
  532:             <span aria-hidden="true">⌕</span>
  533: 
  534:             <h5>No available matches</h5>
  535: 
  536:             <p>
  537:               Change the search or filters, or create a new Skill Library
  538:               record.
  539:             </p>
  540:           </div>
  541:         )}
  542:       </section>
  543: 
  544:       {/*
  545:        * =========================================
  546:        * Create Skill
  547:        * =========================================
  548:        */}
  549: 
  550:       {canCreateSkill && (
  551:         <section className="skill-selector-create">
  552:           <header>
  553:             <span>New Library Skill</span>
  554: 
  555:             <h4>Create “{query.trim()}”</h4>
  556: 
  557:             <p>
  558:               This creates one reusable Skill record. The current Experience,
  559:               Résumé, or Portfolio will store a relationship to that record.
  560:             </p>
  561:           </header>
  562: 
  563:           <div className="skill-selector-create-fields">
  564:             <div>
  565:               <label htmlFor={`${selectorId}-new-category`}>Category</label>
  566: 
  567:               <select
  568:                 id={`${selectorId}-new-category`}
  569:                 value={newSkillCategory}
  570:                 onChange={(event) => setNewSkillCategory(event.target.value)}
  571:                 disabled={isWorking}
  572:               >
  573:                 {SKILL_CATEGORY_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
  574:                   <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
  575:                     {option.label}
  576:                   </option>
  577:                 ))}
  578:               </select>
  579:             </div>
  580: 
  581:             <div>
  582:               <label htmlFor={`${selectorId}-new-type`}>Skill Type</label>
  583: 
  584:               <select
  585:                 id={`${selectorId}-new-type`}
  586:                 value={newSkillType}
  587:                 onChange={(event) => setNewSkillType(event.target.value)}
  588:                 disabled={isWorking}
  589:               >

  593:                   </option>
  594:                 ))}
  595:               </select>
  596:             </div>
  597:           </div>
  598: 
  599:           <button
  600:             type="button"
  601:             onClick={handleCreateSkill}
  602:             disabled={isWorking}
  603:           >
  604:             {status === "loading"
  605:               ? "Creating Skill..."
  606:               : "Create and Select Skill"}
  607:           </button>
  608:         </section>
  609:       )}
  610:     </section>
  611:   );
  612: }
  613: 
  614: export default SkillSelector;

   72: /*
   73:  * =========================================
   74:  * Primitive Schemas
   75:  * =========================================
   76:  */
   77: 
   78: const skillIdSchema = z
   79:   .string()
   80:   .trim()
   81:   .min(1, "A skill ID is required.")
   82:   .max(200, "Skill ID cannot exceed 200 characters.");
   83: 
   84: const skillNameSchema = z
   85:   .string()
   86:   .trim()
   87:   .min(2, "Skill name must contain at least two characters.")
   88:   .max(150, "Skill name cannot exceed 150 characters.")
   89:   .refine(
   90:     (value) => /[\p{L}\p{N}]/u.test(value),
   91:     "Skill name must contain letters or numbers.",
   92:   );
   93: 
   94: const aliasSchema = z
   95:   .string()
   96:   .trim()
   97:   .min(1, "Skill alias cannot be empty.")
   98:   .max(150, "Skill alias cannot exceed 150 characters.");
   99: 
  100: const yearMonthSchema = z
  101:   .string()
  102:   .trim()
  103:   .regex(/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/, "Enter a valid month and year.");
  104: 
  105: const optionalYearMonthSchema = z.union([z.literal(""), yearMonthSchema]);
  106: 
  107: const nullableYearsSchema = z
  108:   .union([z.number(), z.string().trim(), z.null()])
  109:   .transform((value, context) => {
  110:     if (value === "" || value === null) {
  111:       return null;
  112:     }
  113: 
  114:     const numericValue = Number(value);
  115: 
  116:     if (!Number.isFinite(numericValue)) {
  117:       context.addIssue({
  118:         code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
  119:         message: "Years of experience must be a number.",
  120:       });
  121: 
  122:       return z.NEVER;
  123:     }
  124: 
  125:     if (numericValue < 0 || numericValue > 80) {
  126:       context.addIssue({
  127:         code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
  128:         message: "Years of experience must be between zero and 80.",
  129:       });
  130: 
  131:       return z.NEVER;
  132:     }
  133: 
  134:     if (Math.round(numericValue * 10) !== numericValue * 10) {
  135:       context.addIssue({
  136:         code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
  137:         message:
  138:           "Years of experience can contain no more than one decimal place.",
  139:       });
  140: 
  141:       return z.NEVER;
  142:     }
  143: 
  144:     return numericValue;
  145:   });
  146: 
  147: /*
  148:  * =========================================
  149:  * Nested Skill Schemas

  152: 
  153: const aliasesSchema = z
  154:   .array(aliasSchema)
  155:   .max(20, "Add no more than 20 aliases.")
  156:   .default([]);
  157: 
  158: const proficiencySchema = z
  159:   .object({
  160:     level: z.enum(SKILL_PROFICIENCY_VALUES).default(""),
  161: 
  162:     yearsOfExperience: nullableYearsSchema.default(null),
  163: 
  164:     lastUsedDate: optionalYearMonthSchema.default(""),
  165:   })
  166:   .default({
  167:     level: "",
  168:     yearsOfExperience: null,
  169:     lastUsedDate: "",
  170:   });
  171: 
  172: const proficiencyUpdateSchema = z.object({
  173:   level: z.enum(SKILL_PROFICIENCY_VALUES).optional(),
  174: 
  175:   yearsOfExperience: nullableYearsSchema.optional(),
  176: 
  177:   lastUsedDate: optionalYearMonthSchema.optional(),
  178: });
  179: 
  180: const languageSchema = z
  181:   .object({
  182:     proficiency: z.enum(LANGUAGE_PROFICIENCY_VALUES).default(""),
  183:   })
  184:   .default({
  185:     proficiency: "",
  186:   });
  187: 
  188: const languageUpdateSchema = z.object({
  189:   proficiency: z.enum(LANGUAGE_PROFICIENCY_VALUES).optional(),
  190: });
  191: 
  192: /*
  193:  * =========================================
  194:  * Cross-Field Validation
  195:  * =========================================
  196:  */
  197: 
  198: function validateSkillRelationships(skill, context) {
  199:   const isLanguageType = skill.type === "language";
  200: 
  201:   const isLanguageCategory = skill.category === "language";
  202: 
  203:   const languageProficiency = skill.language?.proficiency || "";
  204: 
  205:   if (isLanguageType && !isLanguageCategory) {
  206:     context.addIssue({
  207:       code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
  208:       path: ["category"],
  209:       message: 'A language skill must use the "Languages" category.',
  210:     });
  211:   }
  212: 
  213:   if (isLanguageCategory && !isLanguageType) {
  214:     context.addIssue({
  215:       code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
  216:       path: ["type"],
  217:       message:
  218:         'A skill in the "Languages" category must use the "Language Skill" type.',
  219:     });
  220:   }
  221: 
  222:   if (!isLanguageType && languageProficiency) {
  223:     context.addIssue({
  224:       code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
  225:       path: ["language", "proficiency"],
  226:       message: "Language proficiency can only be assigned to a language skill.",
  227:     });
  228:   }
  229: 
  230:   const normalizedName = skill.name
  231:     .normalize("NFKC")
  232:     .trim()
  233:     .toLocaleLowerCase()
  234:     .replace(/[._/\\-]+/g, " ")
  235:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  236: 
  237:   const seenAliases = new Set();
  238: 
  239:   skill.aliases.forEach((alias, index) => {
  240:     const normalizedAlias = alias
  241:       .normalize("NFKC")
  242:       .trim()
  243:       .toLocaleLowerCase()
  244:       .replace(/[._/\\-]+/g, " ")
  245:       .replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  246: 
  247:     if (normalizedAlias === normalizedName) {
  248:       context.addIssue({
  249:         code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
  250:         path: ["aliases", index],
  251:         message: "An alias cannot be the same as the primary skill name.",
  252:       });
  253:     }
  254: 
  255:     if (seenAliases.has(normalizedAlias)) {
  256:       context.addIssue({
  257:         code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
  258:         path: ["aliases", index],
  259:         message: "This skill alias has already been added.",
  260:       });
  261:     }
  262: 
  263:     seenAliases.add(normalizedAlias);
  264:   });
  265: }
  266: 
  267: /*
  268:  * =========================================
  269:  * Create Skill
  270:  * =========================================
  271:  */
  272: 
  273: const createSkillBaseSchema = z.object({
  274:   name: skillNameSchema,
  275: 
  276:   category: z.enum(SKILL_CATEGORY_VALUES).default("other"),
  277: 
  278:   type: z.enum(SKILL_TYPE_VALUES).default("professional"),
  279: 
  280:   description: z.string().trim().max(1500).default(""),
  281: 
  282:   aliases: aliasesSchema,
  283: 
  284:   proficiency: proficiencySchema,
  285: 
  286:   language: languageSchema,
  287: 
  288:   source: z.enum(SKILL_SOURCE_VALUES).default("manual"),
  289: 
  290:   sourceContext: z.string().trim().max(100).default(""),
  291: 
  292:   notes: z.string().trim().max(2000).default(""),
  293: 
  294:   status: z.enum(SKILL_STATUS_VALUES).default("active"),
  295: });
  296: 
  297: export const createSkillSchema = createSkillBaseSchema.superRefine(
  298:   validateSkillRelationships,
  299: );

  312:   type: z.enum(SKILL_TYPE_VALUES).optional(),

  358:   skillId: skillIdSchema,
  359: });
  360: 
  361: /*
  362:  * =========================================
  363:  * List Query
  364:  * =========================================
  365:  */
  366: 
  367: function parseBooleanQuery(value) {
  368:   if (value === true || value === "true" || value === "1") {
  369:     return true;
  370:   }
  371: 
  372:   if (
  373:     value === false ||
  374:     value === "false" ||
  375:     value === "0" ||
  376:     value === undefined
  377:   ) {
  378:     return false;
  379:   }
  380: 
  381:   return value;
  382: }
  383: 
  384: export const skillListQuerySchema = z.object({
  385:   includeArchived: z.preprocess(parseBooleanQuery, z.boolean()).default(false),
  386: 
  387:   category: z
  388:     .union([z.literal("all"), z.enum(SKILL_CATEGORY_VALUES)])
  389:     .default("all"),
  390: 
  391:   type: z.union([z.literal("all"), z.enum(SKILL_TYPE_VALUES)]).default("all"),
  392: 
  393:   query: z.string().trim().max(200).default(""),
  394: 
  395:   sort: z
  396:     .enum([
  397:       "name-ascending",
  398:       "name-descending",
  399:       "recently-updated",
  400:       "oldest-updated",
  401:     ])
  402:     .default("name-ascending"),
  403: });
  404: 
  405: /*
  406:  * =========================================
  407:  * Skill Name Resolution
  408:  * =========================================
  409:  */
  410: 
  411: export const skillNameQuerySchema = z.object({
  412:   name: skillNameSchema,
  413: 
  414:   excludeSkillId: z.string().trim().max(200).default(""),
  415: });
  416: 
  417: /*
  418:  * =========================================
  419:  * Find or Create
  420:  * =========================================
  421:  */
  422: 
  423: export const findOrCreateSkillSchema = z.object({
  424:   skill: createSkillBaseSchema,
  425: 
  426:   restoreArchived: z.boolean().default(true),
  427: });
  428: 
  429: /*
  430:  * =========================================
  431:  * Collection Replacement
  432:  * =========================================
  433:  */
  434: 
  435: export const replaceSkillsSchema = z.object({
  436:   skills: z
  437:     .array(createSkillBaseSchema)
  438:     .max(1000, "The Skill Library is too large."),
  439: });
  440: 
  441: /*
  442:  * =========================================
  443:  * Validation Error Formatter
  444:  * =========================================
  445:  */
  446: 
  447: export function formatSkillSchemaErrors(error) {
  448:   if (!error?.issues) {
  449:     return [];
  450:   }
  451: 
  452:   return error.issues.map((issue) => ({
  453:     field: issue.path.join("."),
  454:     message: issue.message,
  455:     code: issue.code,
  456:   }));
  457: }

    1: const SERVER_OWNED_EXPERIENCE_FIELDS = [
    2:   "userId",
    3:   "user_id",
    4:   "createdAt",
    5:   "updatedAt",
    6:   "rowVersion",
    7:   "recordStatus",
    8:   "record_status",
    9: ];
   10: 
   11: export function prepareExperienceApiPayload(experience) {
   12:   if (
   13:     !experience ||
   14:     typeof experience !== "object" ||
   15:     Array.isArray(experience)
   16:   ) {
   17:     throw new TypeError("A valid Experience object is required.");
   18:   }
   19: 
   20:   const payload = structuredClone(experience);
   21: 
   22:   for (const field of SERVER_OWNED_EXPERIENCE_FIELDS) {
   23:     delete payload[field];
   24:   }
   25: 
   26:   return payload;
   27: }
   28: 
   29: export function getExperienceRowVersion(experience) {
   30:   const rowVersion = experience?.rowVersion;
   31: 
   32:   if (
   33:     typeof rowVersion !== "string" ||
   34:     !/^[A-Za-z0-9+/]{10}[AEIMQUYcgkosw048]=$/.test(rowVersion)
   35:   ) {
   36:     throw new TypeError(
   37:       "A valid Experience rowVersion is required.",
   38:     );
   39:   }
   40: 
   41:   return rowVersion;
   42: }

    1: import {

   14: const EXPERIENCE_ENDPOINT = "/experiences";
   15: 
   16: function experiencePath(experienceId) {
   17:   if (
   18:     typeof experienceId !== "string" ||
   19:     !experienceId.trim() ||
   20:     experienceId.trim().length > 200
   21:   ) {
   22:     throw new TypeError("A valid Experience ID is required.");
   23:   }
   24: 
   25:   return `${EXPERIENCE_ENDPOINT}/${encodeURIComponent(
   26:     experienceId.trim(),
   27:   )}`;
   28: }
   29: 
   30: function requireRowVersion(rowVersion) {
   31:   return getExperienceRowVersion({ rowVersion });
   32: }
   33: 
   34: export async function fetchExperiences(options = {}) {
   35:   const response = await apiGet(EXPERIENCE_ENDPOINT, options);
   36: 
   37:   return response.data.experiences;
   38: }
   39: 
   40: export async function fetchExperienceById(
   41:   experienceId,
   42:   options = {},
   43: ) {
   44:   const response = await apiGet(
   45:     experiencePath(experienceId),
   46:     options,
   47:   );
   48: 
   49:   return response.data.experience;
   50: }
   51: 
   52: export async function createExperienceApi(
   53:   experience,
   54:   options = {},
   55: ) {
   56:   const response = await apiPost(
   57:     EXPERIENCE_ENDPOINT,
   58:     prepareExperienceApiPayload(experience),
   59:     options,
   60:   );
   61: 
   62:   return response.data.experience;
   63: }
   64: 
   65: export async function updateExperienceApi(
   66:   experienceId,
   67:   experience,
   68:   rowVersion,
   69:   options = {},
   70: ) {
   71:   const response = await apiPut(
   72:     experiencePath(experienceId),
   73:     {
   74:       experience: prepareExperienceApiPayload(experience),
   75:       rowVersion: requireRowVersion(rowVersion),
   76:     },
   77:     options,
   78:   );
   79: 
   80:   return response.data.experience;
   81: }
   82: 
   83: export async function setExperienceArchivedApi(
   84:   experienceId,
   85:   archived,
   86:   rowVersion,
   87:   options = {},
   88: ) {
   89:   if (typeof archived !== "boolean") {
   90:     throw new TypeError("archived must be a boolean.");
   91:   }
   92: 
   93:   const response = await apiPatch(
   94:     `${experiencePath(experienceId)}/archive`,
   95:     {
   96:       archived,
   97:       rowVersion: requireRowVersion(rowVersion),
   98:     },
   99:     options,
  100:   );
  101: 
  102:   return response.data.experience;
  103: }
  104: 
  105: export async function deleteExperienceApi(
  106:   experienceId,
  107:   rowVersion,
  108:   options = {},
  109: ) {
  110:   const response = await apiDelete(
  111:     experiencePath(experienceId),
  112:     {
  113:       ...options,
  114:       body: {
  115:         rowVersion: requireRowVersion(rowVersion),
  116:       },
  117:     },
  118:   );
  119: 
  120:   return response.data;
  121: }

    1: import { updateExperienceModel } from "../../models/experienceModel.js";

    4: 
    5: import * as database from "./experienceDatabaseService.js";
    6: 
    7: /*
    8:  * Compatibility layer for the existing React Experience context.
    9:  *
   10:  * Not connected to ExperienceDataContext yet.
   11:  * Does not access localStorage.
   12:  *
   13:  * The caller supplies the collection currently loaded by React.
   14:  * Its rowVersion is deliberately used without refreshing it first,
   15:  * so stale writes produce a concurrency conflict.
   16:  */
   17: 
   18: function requireCollection(experiences) {
   19:   if (!Array.isArray(experiences)) {
   20:     throw new TypeError("The loaded Experience collection is required.");
   21:   }
   22: 
   23:   return experiences;
   24: }
   25: 
   26: function findLoadedExperience(experiences, experienceId) {
   27:   const collection = requireCollection(experiences);
   28: 
   29:   const experience = collection.find(
   30:     (item) => item?.id === experienceId,
   31:   );
   32: 
   33:   if (!experience) {
   34:     const error = new Error("The requested Experience is not loaded.");
   35: 
   36:     error.name = "ExperienceServiceError";
   37:     error.code = "EXPERIENCE_NOT_LOADED";
   38:     error.status = 404;
   39: 
   40:     throw error;
   41:   }
   42: 
   43:   getExperienceRowVersion(experience);
   44: 
   45:   return experience;
   46: }
   47: 
   48: function requireUpdates(updates) {
   49:   if (
   50:     !updates ||
   51:     typeof updates !== "object" ||
   52:     Array.isArray(updates)
   53:   ) {
   54:     throw new TypeError("Experience updates must be an object.");
   55:   }
   56: 
   57:   if (
   58:     Object.hasOwn(updates, "id") ||
   59:     Object.hasOwn(updates, "rowVersion") ||
   60:     Object.hasOwn(updates, "createdAt") ||
   61:     Object.hasOwn(updates, "updatedAt")
   62:   ) {
   63:     throw new TypeError(
   64:       "Experience updates cannot override server-owned fields.",
   65:     );
   66:   }
   67: 
   68:   return updates;
   69: }
   70: 
   71: export async function getExperiences(options = {}) {
   72:   return database.getExperiences(options);
   73: }
   74: 
   75: export async function getExperienceById(experienceId) {
   76:   return database.getExperienceById(experienceId);
   77: }
   78: 
   79: export async function createExperience(experienceData) {
   80:   assertValidExperience(experienceData);
   81: 
   82:   return database.createExperience(experienceData);
   83: }
   84: 
   85: export async function updateExperience(
   86:   experiences,
   87:   experienceId,
   88:   updates,
   89: ) {
   90:   const current = findLoadedExperience(experiences, experienceId);
   91: 
   92:   requireUpdates(updates);
   93: 
   94:   const updated = updateExperienceModel(current, updates);
   95: 
   96:   assertValidExperience(updated);
   97: 
   98:   return database.updateExperience(experienceId, updated);
   99: }
  100: 
  101: export async function archiveExperience(experiences, experienceId) {
  102:   const current = findLoadedExperience(experiences, experienceId);
  103: 
  104:   return database.archiveExperience(experienceId, current);
  105: }
  106: 
  107: export async function restoreExperience(experiences, experienceId) {

  112:

   25: function requireExperience(experience) {
   26:   if (
   27:     !experience ||
   28:     typeof experience !== "object" ||
   29:     Array.isArray(experience)
   30:   ) {
   31:     throw new TypeError("A complete Experience record is required.");
   32:   }
   33:

   35: }

   41: function requireMatchingId(experienceId, experience) {
   42:   requireExperience(experience);
   43: 
   44:   if (experience.id !== experienceId) {
   45:     throw new TypeError("Experience ID does not match the record.");
   46:   }
   47: }

   50:   includeArchived = false,
   51: } = {}) {

   55:     throw new TypeError("The Experience API returned an invalid collection.");
   56:   }
   57: 
   58:   return includeArchived
   59:     ? experiences
   60:     : experiences.filter((item) => item.status !== "archived");
   61: }

   16: 
   17: export const AUTH_SESSION_INVALID_EVENT =
   18:   "portfolio-platform:auth-session-invalid";
   19: 
   20: const INVALID_SESSION_CODES = new Set([
   21:   "AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED",
   22:   "SESSION_REFRESH_TOKEN_INVALID",
   23:   "SESSION_NOT_FOUND",
   24:   "SESSION_REFRESH_CONFLICT",

   27: /*
   28:  * =========================================
   29:  * Invalid Session Notification
   30:  * =========================================
   31:  */
   32: 
   33: function notifyInvalidAuthenticationSession(error) {
   34:   if (
   35:     typeof window === "undefined" ||
   36:     (Number(error?.status) !== 401 &&
   37:       error?.code !== "SESSION_REFRESH_CONFLICT")
   38:   ) {
   39:     return;
   40:   }
   41: 
   42:   if (!INVALID_SESSION_CODES.has(error?.code)) {
   43:     return;
   44:   }
   45: 
   46:   window.dispatchEvent(
   47:     new CustomEvent(AUTH_SESSION_INVALID_EVENT, {
   48:       detail: {
   49:         status: error.status,
   50: 
   51:         code: error.code,
   52: 
   53:         message: error.message,
   54:       },
   55:     }),
   56:   );
   57: }
