   59: /*
   60:  * VITE_API_BASE_URL may be configured as:
   61:  *
   62:  * http://localhost:5000
   63:  *
   64:  * or:
   65:  *
   66:  * http://localhost:5000/api
   67:  *
   68:  * Both formats are supported.
   69:  */
   70: 
   71: function normalizeApiBaseUrl(value) {
   72:   const configuredValue = typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
   73: 
   74:   const baseUrl = (configuredValue || DEFAULT_API_ORIGIN).replace(/\/+$/, "");
   75: 
   76:   return /\/api$/i.test(baseUrl) ? baseUrl : `${baseUrl}/api`;
   77: }
   78: 
   79: export const API_BASE_URL = normalizeApiBaseUrl(
   80:   import.meta.env?.VITE_API_BASE_URL,
   81: );
   82: 
   83: /*
   84:  * =========================================
   85:  * API Error
   86:  * =========================================
   87:  */
   88: 
   89: export class ApiError extends Error {
   90:   constructor({
   91:     message,
   92:     status = 0,
   93:     code = "API_REQUEST_FAILED",
   94:     fieldErrors = {},
   95:     errors = [],
   96:     details = null,
   97:     method = "",
   98:     url = "",
   99:     cause,
  100:   }) {
  101:     super(message || "The request could not be completed.", {
  102:       cause,
  103:     });
  104: 
  105:     this.name = "ApiError";
  106: 
  107:     this.status = Number(status) || 0;
  108: 
  109:     this.code = code || "API_REQUEST_FAILED";
  110: 
  111:     this.fieldErrors =
  112:       fieldErrors && typeof fieldErrors === "object" ? fieldErrors : {};
  113: 
  114:     this.errors = Array.isArray(errors) ? errors : [];
  115: 
  116:     this.details = details || null;
  117: 
  118:     this.method = method;
  119: 
  120:     this.url = url;
  121:   }
  122: }
  123: 
  124: /*
  125:  * =========================================
  126:  * Endpoint Normalization
  127:  * =========================================
  128:  */
  129: 
  130: function normalizeEndpoint(endpoint) {
  131:   if (typeof endpoint !== "string" || !endpoint.trim()) {
  132:     throw new TypeError("A relative API endpoint is required.");
  133:   }
  134: 
  135:   const normalizedEndpoint = endpoint.trim();
  136: 
  137:   /*
  138:    * Prevent accidental cookie-bearing
  139:    * requests to arbitrary external origins.
  140:    */
  141: 
  142:   if (/^[a-z][a-z\d+\-.]*:\/\//i.test(normalizedEndpoint)) {
  143:     throw new TypeError("API endpoints must be relative paths.");
  144:   }
  145: 
  146:   return normalizedEndpoint.startsWith("/")
  147:     ? normalizedEndpoint
  148:     : `/${normalizedEndpoint}`;
  149: }
  150: 
  151: /*
  152:  * =========================================
  153:  * Query Parameters
  154:  * =========================================
  155:  */
  156: 
  157: function appendQueryParameters(url, query) {
  158:   if (!query || typeof query !== "object") {
  159:     return url;
  160:   }
  161: 
  162:   const requestUrl = new URL(url);
  163: 
  164:   for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(query)) {
  165:     if (value === undefined || value === null || value === "") {
  166:       continue;
  167:     }
  168: 
  169:     if (Array.isArray(value)) {
  170:       for (const item of value) {
  171:         requestUrl.searchParams.append(name, String(item));
  172:       }
  173: 
  174:       continue;
  175:     }
  176: 
  177:     requestUrl.searchParams.set(name, String(value));
  178:   }
  179: 
  180:   return requestUrl.toString();
  181: }
  182: 
  183: /*
  184:  * =========================================
  185:  * Request Body Preparation
  186:  * =========================================
  187:  */
  188: 
  189: function isFormData(value) {
  190:   return typeof FormData !== "undefined" && value instanceof FormData;
  191: }
  192: 
  193: function isUrlSearchParams(value) {
  194:   return (
  195:     typeof URLSearchParams !== "undefined" && value instanceof URLSearchParams
  196:   );
  197: }
  198: 
  199: function isBlob(value) {
  200:   return typeof Blob !== "undefined" && value instanceof Blob;
  201: }
  202: 
  203: function shouldSerializeAsJson(body) {
  204:   return (
  205:     body !== undefined &&
  206:     body !== null &&
  207:     typeof body === "object" &&
  208:     !isFormData(body) &&
  209:     !isUrlSearchParams(body) &&
  210:     !isBlob(body) &&
  211:     !(body instanceof ArrayBuffer)
  212:   );
  213: }
  214: 
  215: function prepareRequestBody({ body, headers }) {
  216:   if (body === undefined || body === null) {
  217:     return undefined;
  218:   }
  219: 
  220:   if (shouldSerializeAsJson(body)) {
  221:     if (!headers.has("Content-Type")) {
  222:       headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json");
  223:     }
  224: 
  225:     return JSON.stringify(body);
  226:   }
  227: 
  228:   /*
  229:    * Never set Content-Type manually for
  230:    * FormData. The browser must add its
  231:    * multipart boundary.
  232:    */
  233: 
  234:   return body;
  235: }
  236: 
  237: /*
  238:  * =========================================
  239:  * Response Parsing
  240:  * =========================================
  241:  */
  242: 
  243: async function parseResponseBody(response) {
  244:   if (response.status === 204 || response.status === 205) {
  245:     return null;
  246:   }
  247: 
  248:   const text = await response.text();
  249: 
  250:   if (!text) {
  251:     return null;
  252:   }
  253: 
  254:   const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") || "";
  255: 
  256:   if (contentType.includes("application/json")) {
  257:     try {
  258:       return JSON.parse(text);
  259:     } catch {
  260:       return {
  261:         message: "The server returned an invalid JSON response.",
  262:       };
  263:     }
  264:   }
  265: 
  266:   /*
  267:    * Some development proxies may omit the
  268:    * JSON content type. Attempt JSON parsing
  269:    * before treating the body as plain text.
  270:    */
  271: 
  272:   try {
  273:     return JSON.parse(text);
  274:   } catch {
  275:     return {
  276:       message: text,
  277:     };
  278:   }
  279: }
  280: 
  281: /*
  282:  * =========================================
  283:  * API Error Creation
  284:  * =========================================
  285:  */
  286: 
  287: function createResponseError({ response, data, method, url }) {
  288:   const error = new ApiError({
  289:     message: data?.message || `The server returned HTTP ${response.status}.`,
  290: 
  291:     status: response.status,
  292: 
  293:     code: data?.code || "API_REQUEST_FAILED",
  294: 
  295:     fieldErrors: data?.fieldErrors,
  296: 
  297:     errors: data?.errors,
  298: 
  299:     details: data?.details,
  300: 
  301:     method,
  302: 
  303:     url,
  304:   });
  305: 
  306:   notifyInvalidAuthenticationSession(error);
  307: 
  308:   return error;
  309: }
  310: 
  311: /*
  312:  * =========================================
  313:  * Timeout and Abort Signal
  314:  * =========================================
  315:  */
  316: 
  317: function createRequestAbortController({ signal, timeoutMs }) {
  318:   const controller = new AbortController();
  319: 
  320:   let wasTimedOut = false;
  321: 
  322:   const handleExternalAbort = () => {
  323:     controller.abort(signal?.reason);
  324:   };
  325: 
  326:   if (signal) {
  327:     if (signal.aborted) {
  328:       handleExternalAbort();
  329:     } else {
  330:       signal.addEventListener("abort", handleExternalAbort, {
  331:         once: true,
  332:       });
  333:     }
  334:   }
  335: 
  336:   const timeoutId = globalThis.setTimeout(() => {
  337:     wasTimedOut = true;
  338: 
  339:     controller.abort();
  340:   }, timeoutMs);
  341: 
  342:   return {
  343:     signal: controller.signal,
  344: 
  345:     wasTimedOut() {
  346:       return wasTimedOut;
  347:     },
  348: 
  349:     cleanup() {
  350:       globalThis.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
  351: 
  352:       signal?.removeEventListener?.("abort", handleExternalAbort);
  353:     },
  354:   };
  355: }
  356: 
  357: /*
  358:  * =========================================
  359:  * API Request
  360:  * =========================================
  361:  */
  362: 
  363: export async function apiRequest(
  364:   endpoint,
  365:   {
  366:     method = "GET",
  367:     body,
  368:     query,
  369:     headers: customHeaders,
  370:     signal,
  371:     timeoutMs = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS,
  372:     cache = "no-store",
  373:   } = {},
  374: ) {
  375:   const normalizedMethod = String(method).trim().toUpperCase() || "GET";
  376: 
  377:   const normalizedEndpoint = normalizeEndpoint(endpoint);
  378: 
  379:   const requestUrl = appendQueryParameters(
  380:     `${API_BASE_URL}${normalizedEndpoint}`,
  381:     query,
  382:   );
  383: 
  384:   const headers = new Headers(customHeaders || {});
  385: 
  386:   if (!headers.has("Accept")) {
  387:     headers.set("Accept", "application/json");
  388:   }
  389: 
  390:   const requestBody = prepareRequestBody({
  391:     body,
  392: 
  393:     headers,
  394:   });
  395: 
  396:   const abortState = createRequestAbortController({
  397:     signal,
  398: 
  399:     timeoutMs: Number(timeoutMs) > 0 ? Number(timeoutMs) : DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS,
  400:   });
  401: 
  402:   try {
  403:     const response = await fetch(requestUrl, {
  404:       method: normalizedMethod,
  405: 
  406:       /*
  407:        * Required for HttpOnly session
  408:        * cookies on cross-origin requests.
  409:        */
  410: 
  411:       credentials: "include",
  412: 
  413:       headers,
  414: 
  415:       body: ["GET", "HEAD"].includes(normalizedMethod)
  416:         ? undefined
  417:         : requestBody,
  418: 
  419:       signal: abortState.signal,
  420: 
  421:       cache,
  422:     });
  423: 
  424:     const data = await parseResponseBody(response);
  425: 
  426:     if (!response.ok) {
  427:       throw createResponseError({
  428:         response,
  429: 
  430:         data,
  431: 
  432:         method: normalizedMethod,
  433: 
  434:         url: requestUrl,
  435:       });
  436:     }
  437: 
  438:     return data;
  439:   } catch (error) {
  440:     if (error instanceof ApiError) {
  441:       throw error;
  442:     }
  443: 
  444:     if (error?.name === "AbortError") {
  445:       throw new ApiError({
  446:         message: abortState.wasTimedOut()
  447:           ? "The server took too long to respond."
  448:           : "The request was canceled.",
  449: 
  450:         status: 0,
  451: 
  452:         code: abortState.wasTimedOut()
  453:           ? "API_REQUEST_TIMEOUT"
  454:           : "API_REQUEST_ABORTED",
  455: 
  456:         method: normalizedMethod,
  457: 
  458:         url: requestUrl,
  459: 
  460:         cause: error,
  461:       });
  462:     }
  463: 
  464:     throw new ApiError({
  465:       message:
  466:         "Unable to connect to the server. Check your connection and try again.",
  467: 
  468:       status: 0,
  469: 
  470:       code: "API_NETWORK_ERROR",
  471: 
  472:       method: normalizedMethod,
  473: 
  474:       url: requestUrl,
  475: 
  476:       cause: error,
  477:     });
  478:   } finally {
  479:     abortState.cleanup();
  480:   }
  481: }
  482: 
  483: /*
  484:  * =========================================
  485:  * Convenience Methods
  486:  * =========================================
  487:  */
  488: 
  489: export function apiGet(endpoint, options = {}) {
  490:   return apiRequest(endpoint, {
  491:     ...options,
  492: 
  493:     method: "GET",
  494:   });
  495: }
  496: 
  497: export function apiPost(endpoint, body, options = {}) {
  498:   return apiRequest(endpoint, {
  499:     ...options,
  500: 
  501:     method: "POST",
  502: 
  503:     body,
  504:   });
  505: }
  506: 
  507: export function apiPut(endpoint, body, options = {}) {
  508:   return apiRequest(endpoint, {
  509:     ...options,
  510: 
  511:     method: "PUT",
  512: 
  513:     body,
  514:   });
  515: }
  516:

  518:   return apiRequest(endpoint, {
  519:     ...options,
  520: 
  521:     method: "PATCH",
  522: 
  523:     body,
  524:   });
  525: }

   10: function requestError(message) {
   11:   const error = new Error(message);
   12:   error.name = "ExperienceRequestError";
   13:   error.code = "EXPERIENCE_REQUEST_INVALID";
   14:   error.status = 400;
   15:   error.publicMessage = message;

   18: 
   19: function requireFields(value, allowedFields) {
   20:   if (
   21:     value === null ||
   22:     typeof value !== "object" ||
   23:     Array.isArray(value)
   24:   ) {
   25:     throw requestError("Request body must be a JSON object.");
   26:   }
   27: 
   28:   const unexpected = Object.keys(value).filter(
   29:     (field) => !allowedFields.includes(field),
   30:   );
   31: 
   32:   if (unexpected.length > 0) {
   33:     throw requestError(
   34:       `Unexpected request fields: ${unexpected.join(", ")}.`,
   35:     );
   36:   }
   37: 
   38:   return value;
   39: }

   44:     Pragma: "no-cache",
   45:     Expires: "0",
   46:   });
   47: 
   48:   return response.status(status).json({
   49:     status: "success",
   50:     message,
   51:     data,
   52:   });
   53: }

    3: const EXPERIENCE_COLUMNS = `
    4:   id,
    5:   user_id,
    6:   position_title,
    7:   organization_name,
    8:   category,
    9:   record_status,
   10:   experience_data,

   16: function mapExperienceRow(row) {
   17:   if (!row) {
   18:     return null;
   19:   }
   20: 
   21:   const experienceData =
   22:     typeof row.experience_data === "string"
   23:       ? JSON.parse(row.experience_data)
   24:       : row.experience_data;
   25: 
   26:   if (
   27:     !experienceData ||
   28:     typeof experienceData !== "object" ||
   29:     Array.isArray(experienceData)
   30:   ) {
   31:     throw new Error("Stored Experience data is invalid.");
   32:   }
   33: 
   34:   return {
   35:     ...experienceData,
   36: 
   37:     // Database columns are authoritative.
   38:     id: row.id,
   39:     position: {
   40:       ...(experienceData.position || {}),
   41:       title: row.position_title,
   42:     },
   43:     organization: {
   44:       ...(experienceData.organization || {}),
   45:       name: row.organization_name,
   46:     },
   47:     category: row.category,
   48:     status: row.record_status,
   49: 
   50:     createdAt: row.created_at,
   51:     updatedAt: row.updated_at,
   52:     rowVersion: Buffer.from(row.row_version).toString("base64"),
   53:   };
   54: }
   55: 
   56: function bindExperienceParameters(request, userId, experience) {
   57:   return request
   58:     .input("id", sql.NVarChar(200), experience.id)
   59:     .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
   60:     .input(
   61:       "positionTitle",
   62:       sql.NVarChar(200),
   63:       experience.position.title,
   64:     )
   65:     .input(
   66:       "organizationName",
   67:       sql.NVarChar(200),
   68:       experience.organization.name,
   69:     )
   70:     .input("category", sql.NVarChar(50), experience.category)
   71:     .input("recordStatus", sql.NVarChar(20), experience.status)
   72:     .input(
   73:       "experienceData",
   74:       sql.NVarChar(sql.MAX),
   75:       JSON.stringify(experience),
   76:     );
   77: }
   78: 
   79: export async function listExperiencesByUser(userId) {
   80:   const pool = await getDatabasePool();
   81: 
   82:   const result = await pool
   83:     .request()
   84:     .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
   85:     .query(`
   86:       SELECT ${EXPERIENCE_COLUMNS}
   87:       FROM dbo.experiences
   88:       WHERE user_id = @userId
   89:       ORDER BY updated_at DESC, id ASC;
   90:     `);
   91: 
   92:   return result.recordset.map(mapExperienceRow);
   93: }

   95: export async function findExperienceById(userId, experienceId) {

   97: 
   98:   const result = await pool
   99:     .request()
  100:     .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)

  102:     .query(`
  103:       SELECT ${EXPERIENCE_COLUMNS}
  104:       FROM dbo.experiences
  105:       WHERE user_id = @userId
  106:         AND id = @id;
  107:     `);
  108: 
  109:   return mapExperienceRow(result.recordset[0]);
  110: }

  112: export async function createExperience(userId, experience) {
  113:   const pool = await getDatabasePool();
  114: 
  115:   await bindExperienceParameters(
  116:     pool.request(),
  117:     userId,
  118:     experience,
  119:   ).query(`
  120:     INSERT INTO dbo.experiences (
  121:       id,
  122:       user_id,
  123:       position_title,
  124:       organization_name,
  125:       category,
  126:       record_status,
  127:       experience_data
  128:     )
  129:     VALUES (
  130:       @id,
  131:       @userId,
  132:       @positionTitle,
  133:       @organizationName,
  134:       @category,
  135:       @recordStatus,
  136:       @experienceData
  137:     );
  138:   `);
  139: 
  140:   return findExperienceById(userId, experience.id);
  141: }
  142: 
  143: export async function updateExperience(
  144:   userId,
  145:   experience,
  146:   expectedRowVersion,
  147: ) {
  148:   const pool = await getDatabasePool();
  149: 
  150:   const request = bindExperienceParameters(
  151:     pool.request(),
  152:     userId,
  153:     experience,
  154:   ).input(
  155:     "expectedRowVersion",
  156:     sql.Binary(8),
  157:     Buffer.from(expectedRowVersion, "base64"),
  158:   );
  159: 
  160:   const result = await request.query(`
  161:     UPDATE dbo.experiences
  162:     SET
  163:       position_title = @positionTitle,
  164:       organization_name = @organizationName,
  165:       category = @category,
  166:       record_status = @recordStatus,
  167:       experience_data = @experienceData,
  168:       updated_at = SYSUTCDATETIME()
  169:     WHERE id = @id
  170:       AND user_id = @userId
  171:       AND row_version = @expectedRowVersion;
  172:   `);
  173: 
  174:   if (result.rowsAffected[0] === 0) {
  175:     return null;
  176:   }
  177: 
  178:   return findExperienceById(userId, experience.id);
  179: }

   72: function isObject(value) {

   74:     typeof value === "object" &&

   78: function createValidationError(errors) {
   79:   const error = new Error("Experience validation failed.");
   80:   error.name = "ExperienceValidationError";
   81:   error.code = "EXPERIENCE_VALIDATION_FAILED";
   82:   error.status = 400;
   83:   error.publicMessage = "Please correct the Experience information.";
   84:   error.errors = errors;
   85:   error.fieldErrors = {};
   86: 
   87:   for (const item of errors) {
   88:     if (!error.fieldErrors[item.field]) {
   89:       error.fieldErrors[item.field] = item.message;
   90:     }
   91:   }
   92: 
   93:   return error;
   94: }
   95: 
   96: function addError(errors, field, code, message) {
   97:   errors.push({ field, code, message });
   98: }

  100: function text(value) {
  101:   return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
  102: }
  103: 
  104: function checkText(errors, field, value, max, required = false) {
  105:   if (value !== undefined && typeof value !== "string") {
  106:     addError(errors, field, "invalid_type", "Must be text.");
  107:     return;
  108:   }
  109: 
  110:   if (required && !text(value)) {
  111:     addError(errors, field, "required", "This field is required.");
  112:   }
  113: 
  114:   if (typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > max) {
  115:     addError(errors, field, "too_long", `Maximum ${max} characters.`);
  116:   }
  117: }
  118: 
  119: function checkObject(errors, source, key) {
  120:   if (!isObject(source[key])) {
  121:     addError(errors, key, "invalid_type", "Must be an object.");
  122:     return {};
  123:   }
  124: 
  125:   return source[key];
  126: }

  140: function validDate(value) {
  141:   const match = typeof value === "string"
  142:     ? value.match(DATE_PATTERN)
  143:     : null;
  144: 
  145:   if (!match) return false;
  146:   if (!match[3]) return true;
  147: 
  148:   const year = Number(match[1]);
  149:   const month = Number(match[2]);
  150:   const day = Number(match[3]);
  151:   const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
  152: 
  153:   return date.getUTCFullYear() === year &&
  154:     date.getUTCMonth() === month - 1 &&
  155:     date.getUTCDate() === day;
  156: }
  157: 
  158: function comparableDate(value, endOfMonth = false) {
  159:   const [year, month, suppliedDay] = value.split("-").map(Number);
  160:   const day = suppliedDay ||
  161:     (endOfMonth ? new Date(Date.UTC(year, month, 0)).getUTCDate() : 1);
  162: 
  163:   return Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day);
  164: }
  165: 
  166: function checkDates(errors, dates) {
  167:   const start = dates.startDate;
  168:   const end = dates.endDate;
  169:   const current = dates.isCurrent;
  170: 
  171:   checkBoolean(errors, "dates.isCurrent", current);
  172: 
  173:   if (!validDate(start)) {
  174:     addError(errors, "dates.startDate", "invalid_date",
  175:       "A valid start date is required.");
  176:   }
  177: 
  178:   if (current === true && text(end)) {
  179:     addError(errors, "dates.endDate", "current_position_has_end_date",
  180:       "A current position cannot have an end date.");
  181:   }
  182: 
  183:   if (current !== true && !text(end)) {
  184:     addError(errors, "dates.endDate", "required",
  185:       "An end date is required for a previous position.");
  186:   } else if (text(end) && !validDate(end)) {
  187:     addError(errors, "dates.endDate", "invalid_date",
  188:       "Invalid end date.");
  189:   }
  190: 
  191:   if (validDate(start) && validDate(end) &&
  192:       comparableDate(end, true) < comparableDate(start)) {
  193:     addError(errors, "dates.endDate", "invalid_date_order",
  194:       "End date cannot precede start date.");
  195:   }
  196: }
  197: 
  198: function checkCollection(errors, experience, name) {
  199:   const values = experience[name];
  200: 
  201:   if (values === undefined) return;
  202: 
  203:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
  204:     addError(errors, name, "invalid_type", "Must be an array.");
  205:     return;
  206:   }
  207: 
  208:   if (values.length > COLLECTION_LIMITS[name]) {
  209:     addError(errors, name, "too_many_items",
  210:       `Maximum ${COLLECTION_LIMITS[name]} items.`);
  211:   }
  212: }
  213: 
  214: function checkUnique(errors, values, field, identity) {
  215:   const seen = new Set();
  216: 
  217:   values.forEach((item, index) => {
  218:     const key = identity(item).trim().toLowerCase();
  219:     if (!key) return;
  220: 
  221:     if (seen.has(key)) {
  222:       addError(errors, `${field}.${index}`, "duplicate",
  223:         "Duplicate entry.");
  224:     }
  225:     seen.add(key);
  226:   });
  227: }
  228: 
  229: function checkRelationships(errors, values, kind) {
  230:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) return;
  231: 
  232:   values.forEach((value, index) => {
  233:     const prefix = `${kind}.${index}`;
  234: 
  235:     if (!isObject(value)) {
  236:       addError(errors, prefix, "invalid_type", "Must be an object.");
  237:       return;
  238:     }
  239: 
  240:     const skillId = text(value.skillId || value.profileSkillId);
  241:     const snapshot = text(
  242:       value.nameSnapshot || value.name || value.label || value.value
  243:     );
  244: 
  245:     if (!skillId && !snapshot) {
  246:       addError(errors, `${prefix}.skillId`, "required",
  247:         "Select a saved skill or provide its name.");
  248:     }
  249: 
  250:     if (skillId && !snapshot) {
  251:       addError(errors, `${prefix}.nameSnapshot`, "required_snapshot",
  252:         "A name snapshot is required.");
  253:     }
  254: 
  255:     for (const field of ["id", "skillId"]) {
  256:       if (value[field] !== undefined) {
  257:         checkText(errors, `${prefix}.${field}`, value[field], 200);
  258:       }
  259:     }
  260: 
  261:     const levelField = kind === "skills" ? "level" : "proficiency";
  262:     const level = value[levelField] ?? "";
  263: 
  264:     checkOption(errors, `${prefix}.${levelField}`, level, PROFICIENCIES);
  265: 
  266:     if (value.order !== undefined &&
  267:         (!Number.isInteger(value.order) || value.order < 0)) {
  268:       addError(errors, `${prefix}.order`, "invalid_order",
  269:         "Order must be a nonnegative integer.");
  270:     }
  271: 
  272:     const limits = kind === "skills"
  273:       ? { nameSnapshot: 150, categorySnapshot: 100,
  274:           typeSnapshot: 100, usageDescription: 500 }
  275:       : { nameSnapshot: 150, name: 150,
  276:           category: 100, usageDescription: 500 };
  277: 
  278:     for (const [field, max] of Object.entries(limits)) {
  279:       checkText(errors, `${prefix}.${field}`, value[field], max);
  280:     }
  281:   });
  282: 
  283:   checkUnique(errors, values, kind, (value) => {
  284:     if (!isObject(value)) return "";
  285:     const id = text(value.skillId || value.profileSkillId);
  286:     const name = text(value.nameSnapshot || value.name);
  287:     return id ? `id:${id}` : `name:${name}`;
  288:   });
  289: }
  290: 
  291: export function parseExperienceInput(value) {
  292:   const errors = [];
  293: 
  294:   if (!isObject(value)) {
  295:     throw createValidationError([{
  296:       field: "experience",
  297:       code: "invalid_type",
  298:       message: "Experience must be an object.",
  299:     }]);
  300:   }
  301: 
  302:   for (const field of SERVER_FIELDS) {
  303:     if (Object.hasOwn(value, field)) {
  304:       addError(errors, field, "server_owned",
  305:         "This field is managed by the server.");
  306:     }
  307:   }
  308: 
  309:   if (typeof value.id !== "string" ||
  310:       value.id.length > 200 || !ID_PATTERN.test(value.id)) {
  311:     addError(errors, "id", "invalid_identifier",
  312:       "A valid Experience ID is required.");
  313:   }
  314: 
  315:   if (value.modelVersion !== undefined &&
  316:       value.modelVersion !== EXPERIENCE_MODEL_VERSION) {
  317:     addError(errors, "modelVersion", "invalid_version",
  318:       "Unsupported Experience model version.");
  319:   }
  320: 
  321:   checkOption(errors, "category", value.category, CATEGORIES);
  322:   checkOption(errors, "status", value.status, STATUSES);
  323: 
  324:   const position = checkObject(errors, value, "position");
  325:   const organization = checkObject(errors, value, "organization");
  326:   const dates = checkObject(errors, value, "dates");
  327: 
  328:   checkOption(errors, "position.employmentType",
  329:     position.employmentType, EMPLOYMENT_TYPES);
  330:   checkOption(errors, "position.workArrangement",
  331:     position.workArrangement, WORK_ARRANGEMENTS);
  332: 
  333:   for (const [field, max] of Object.entries(LIMITS)) {
  334:     const [section, property] = field.split(".");
  335:     const fieldValue = property ? value[section]?.[property] : value[field];
  336: 
  337:     checkText(errors, field, fieldValue, max,
  338:       field === "position.title" || field === "organization.name");
  339:   }
  340: 
  341:   if (value.organization?.website) {
  342:     const website = text(value.organization.website);
  343: 
  344:     try {
  345:       const normalized = /^https?:\/\//i.test(website)
  346:         ? website : `https://${website}`;
  347:       const url = new URL(normalized);
  348: 
  349:       if (!["http:", "https:"].includes(url.protocol) || !url.hostname) {
  350:         throw new Error("Invalid URL");
  351:       }
  352:     } catch {
  353:       addError(errors, "organization.website", "invalid_url",
  354:         "A valid website is required.");
  355:     }
  356:   }
  357: 
  358:   checkDates(errors, dates);
  359: 
  360:   for (const name of Object.keys(COLLECTION_LIMITS)) {
  361:     checkCollection(errors, value, name);
  362:   }
  363: 
  364:   for (const kind of ["responsibilities", "achievements"]) {
  365:     const values = value[kind];
  366:     if (!Array.isArray(values)) continue;
  367: 
  368:     values.forEach((item, index) => {
  369:       const itemText = typeof item === "string"
  370:         ? item : isObject(item) ? item.text : "";
  371: 
  372:       checkText(errors, `${kind}.${index}.text`, itemText,
  373:         kind === "responsibilities" ? 1500 : 1500, true);
  374: 
  375:       if (isObject(item)) {
  376:         checkText(errors, `${kind}.${index}.id`, item.id, 200);
  377: 
  378:         if (item.order !== undefined &&
  379:             (!Number.isInteger(item.order) || item.order < 0)) {
  380:           addError(errors, `${kind}.${index}.order`, "invalid_order",
  381:             "Order must be a nonnegative integer.");
  382:         }
  383: 
  384:         if (kind === "achievements") {
  385:           checkText(errors, `${kind}.${index}.metric`, item.metric, 300);
  386:         }
  387:       }
  388:     });
  389: 
  390:     checkUnique(errors, values, kind, (item) =>
  391:       typeof item === "string" ? item : text(item?.text));
  392:   }
  393: 
  394:   checkRelationships(errors, value.skills, "skills");
  395:   checkRelationships(errors, value.technologies, "technologies");
  396: 
  397:   if (Array.isArray(value.relatedProjectIds)) {
  398:     value.relatedProjectIds.forEach((id, index) => {
  399:       checkText(errors, `relatedProjectIds.${index}`, id, 200, true);
  400:     });
  401: 
  402:     checkUnique(errors, value.relatedProjectIds,
  403:       "relatedProjectIds", (id) => text(id));
  404:   }
  405: 
  406:   for (const name of ["location", "leadership",
  407:     "privateInformation", "visibility", "overviewMeta"]) {
  408:     if (value[name] !== undefined && !isObject(value[name])) {
  409:       addError(errors, name, "invalid_type", "Must be an object.");
  410:     }
  411:   }
  412: 
  413:   if (isObject(value.overviewMeta)) {
  414:     const meta = value.overviewMeta;
  415: 
  416:     if (meta.source !== undefined) {
  417:       checkOption(errors, "overviewMeta.source",
  418:         meta.source, OVERVIEW_SOURCES);
  419:     }
  420: 
  421:     checkText(errors, "overviewMeta.status", meta.status, 50);
  422: 
  423:     if (meta.generatedAt !== undefined &&
  424:         meta.generatedAt !== null) {
  425:       checkText(errors, "overviewMeta.generatedAt",
  426:         meta.generatedAt, 100);
  427:     }
  428: 
  429:     checkText(errors, "overviewMeta.contextFingerprint",
  430:       meta.contextFingerprint, 300);
  431: 
  432:     checkBoolean(errors, "overviewMeta.isStale", meta.isStale);
  433:   }
  434: 
  435:   const leadership = isObject(value.leadership) ? value.leadership : {};
  436:   checkBoolean(errors, "leadership.hasLeadershipResponsibilities",
  437:     leadership.hasLeadershipResponsibilities);
  438: 
  439:   const people = leadership.peopleManaged;
  440:   if (people !== undefined && people !== null && people !== "") {
  441:     const number = Number(people);
  442: 
  443:     if (!Number.isInteger(number) || number < 0 || number > 100000) {
  444:       addError(errors, "leadership.peopleManaged", "invalid_number",
  445:         "People managed must be between 0 and 100000.");
  446:     }
  447:   }
  448: 
  449:   if (isObject(value.visibility)) {
  450:     for (const [key, enabled] of Object.entries(value.visibility)) {
  451:       if (!VISIBILITY_FIELDS.has(key)) {
  452:         addError(errors, `visibility.${key}`, "unsupported_field",
  453:           "Unsupported visibility setting.");
  454:         continue;
  455:       }
  456: 
  457:       checkBoolean(errors, `visibility.${key}`, enabled);
  458:     }
  459:   }
  460: 
  461:   const hasOverview = Boolean(text(value.overview));
  462:   const hasResponsibility = Array.isArray(value.responsibilities) &&
  463:     value.responsibilities.some((item) =>
  464:       Boolean(text(typeof item === "string" ? item : item?.text)));
  465:   const hasAchievement = Array.isArray(value.achievements) &&
  466:     value.achievements.some((item) =>
  467:       Boolean(text(typeof item === "string" ? item : item?.text)));
  468: 
  469:   if (!hasOverview && !hasResponsibility && !hasAchievement) {
  470:     addError(errors, "overview", "insufficient_content",
  471:       "Provide an overview, responsibility, or achievement.");
  472:   }
  473: 
  474:   if (errors.length) {
  475:     throw createValidationError(errors);
  476:   }
  477: 
  478:   return structuredClone(value);
  479: }
  480: 
  481: export function parseExperienceRowVersion(value) {
  482:   if (typeof value !== "string" ||
  483:       !/^[A-Za-z0-9+/]{10}[AEIMQUYcgkosw048]=$/.test(value) ||
  484:       Buffer.from(value, "base64").length !== 8) {
  485:     throw createValidationError([{
  486:       field: "rowVersion",
  487:       code: "EXPERIENCE_VERSION_INVALID",
  488:       message: "A valid Experience version is required.",
  489:     }]);
  490:   }
  491: 
  492:   return value;
  493: }

   25:   message,
   26:   code,
   27:   status,
   28:   publicMessage,

   30:   const error = new Error(message);
   31: 
   32:   error.name = "ExperienceServiceError";

   40: function assertUserId(userId) {
   41:   if (typeof userId !== "string" || !userId.trim()) {
   42:     throw createExperienceError({
   43:       message: "An authenticated user ID is required.",
   44:       code: "EXPERIENCE_AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED",
   45:       status: 401,
   46:       publicMessage:
   47:         "You must sign in to access your Experiences.",
   48:     });
   49:   }

   85:     status: 409,

   95:     code: "EXPERIENCE_ID_MISMATCH",

   97:     publicMessage:

  102: function withoutServerFields(experience) {
  103:   const result = { ...experience };
  104: 
  105:   for (const field of SERVER_OWNED_FIELDS) {
  106:     delete result[field];
  107:   }
  108: 
  109:   return result;
  110: }

  117:   userId,

  121:     assertUserId(userId),
  122:     assertExperienceId(experienceId),

  132: export async function createAuthenticatedExperience({
  133:   userId,
  134:   experience,
  135: }) {
  136:   const authenticatedUserId = assertUserId(userId);
  137: 
  138:   // Reject invalid client data before accessing the database.
  139:   const validatedExperience = parseExperienceInput(experience);
  140: 
  141:   try {
  142:     return await createExperience(
  143:       authenticatedUserId,
  144:       validatedExperience,
  145:     );
  146:   } catch (error) {
  147:     if (error.number === 2627 || error.number === 2601) {
  148:       throw createExperienceError({
  149:         message: "Experience ID already exists.",
  150:         code: "EXPERIENCE_ALREADY_EXISTS",
  151:         status: 409,
  152:         publicMessage:
  153:           "An Experience with this identifier already exists.",
  154:       });
  155:     }
  156: 
  157:     throw error;
  158:   }
  159: }

  161: export async function updateAuthenticatedExperience({
  162:   userId,
  163:   experienceId,
  164:   experience,
  165:   rowVersion,
  166: }) {
  167:   const authenticatedUserId = assertUserId(userId);
  168:   const normalizedExperienceId = assertExperienceId(experienceId);
  169: 
  170:   const expectedRowVersion =
  171:     parseExperienceRowVersion(rowVersion);
  172: 
  173:   const validatedExperience =
  174:     parseExperienceInput(experience);
  175: 
  176:   if (validatedExperience.id !== normalizedExperienceId) {
  177:     throw experienceIdMismatch();
  178:   }
  179: 
  180:   const existingExperience = await findExperienceById(
  181:     authenticatedUserId,
  182:     normalizedExperienceId,
  183:   );
  184: 
  185:   if (!existingExperience) {
  186:     throw experienceNotFound();
  187:   }
  188: 
  189:   const updatedExperience = await updateExperience(
  190:     authenticatedUserId,
  191:     validatedExperience,
  192:     expectedRowVersion,
  193:   );
  194: 
  195:   if (!updatedExperience) {
  196:     throw experienceVersionConflict();
  197:   }
  198: 
  199:   return updatedExperience;
  200: }

  202: export async function setAuthenticatedExperienceArchiveStatus({
  203:   userId,
  204:   experienceId,
  205:   rowVersion,
  206:   archived,
  207: }) {
  208:   const authenticatedUserId = assertUserId(userId);
  209:   const normalizedExperienceId = assertExperienceId(experienceId);
  210: 
  211:   const expectedRowVersion =
  212:     parseExperienceRowVersion(rowVersion);
  213: 
  214:   if (typeof archived !== "boolean") {
  215:     throw createExperienceError({
  216:       message: "Archive status must be a boolean.",
  217:       code: "EXPERIENCE_ARCHIVE_STATUS_INVALID",
  218:       status: 400,
  219:       publicMessage: "A valid archive status is required.",
  220:     });
  221:   }
  222: 
  223:   const existingExperience = await findExperienceById(
  224:     authenticatedUserId,
  225:     normalizedExperienceId,
  226:   );
  227: 
  228:   if (!existingExperience) {
  229:     throw experienceNotFound();
  230:   }
  231: 
  232:   // Preserve editable Experience content while removing
  233:   // database-managed metadata.
  234:   const editableExperience =
  235:     withoutServerFields(existingExperience);
  236: 
  237:   const validatedExperience = parseExperienceInput({
  238:     ...editableExperience,
  239:     status: archived ? "archived" : "active",
  240:   });
  241: 
  242:   // Always use the caller's version for concurrency checks.
  243:   const updatedExperience = await updateExperience(
  244:     authenticatedUserId,
  245:     validatedExperience,
  246:     expectedRowVersion,
  247:   );
  248: 
  249:   if (!updatedExperience) {
  250:     throw experienceVersionConflict();
  251:   }
  252: 
  253:   return updatedExperience;
  254: }

  256: export async function deleteAuthenticatedExperience({
  257:   userId,
  258:   experienceId,
  259:   rowVersion,
  260: }) {
  261:   const authenticatedUserId = assertUserId(userId);
  262:   const normalizedExperienceId = assertExperienceId(experienceId);
  263: 
  264:   const expectedRowVersion =
  265:     parseExperienceRowVersion(rowVersion);
  266: 
  267:   const existingExperience = await findExperienceById(
  268:     authenticatedUserId,
  269:     normalizedExperienceId,
  270:   );
  271: 
  272:   if (!existingExperience) {
  273:     throw experienceNotFound();
  274:   }
  275: 
  276:   const deleted = await deleteExperience(
  277:     authenticatedUserId,
  278:     normalizedExperienceId,
  279:     expectedRowVersion,
  280:   );
  281: 
  282:   if (!deleted) {
  283:     throw experienceVersionConflict();
  284:   }
  285: 
  286:   return { deleted: true };
  287: }

   52: 
   53:     CONSTRAINT CK_experiences_position_not_blank
   54:         CHECK (LEN(LTRIM(RTRIM(position_title))) > 0),
   55: 
   56:     CONSTRAINT CK_experiences_organization_not_blank
   57:         CHECK (LEN(LTRIM(RTRIM(organization_name))) > 0),
   58: 
   59:     CONSTRAINT CK_experiences_category
   60:         CHECK (
   61:             category IN (
   62:                 N'employment',
   63:                 N'internship',
   64:                 N'freelance',
   65:                 N'self-employment',
   66:                 N'volunteer',
   67:                 N'leadership',
   68:                 N'research',
   69:                 N'teaching',
   70:                 N'military',
   71:                 N'other'
   72:             )
   73:         ),
   74: 
   75:     CONSTRAINT CK_experiences_record_status
   76:         CHECK (
   77:             record_status IN (
   78:                 N'active',
   79:                 N'archived'
   80:             )
   81:         ),
   82: 
   83:     CONSTRAINT CK_experiences_data_json
   84:         CHECK (ISJSON(experience_data) = 1),
   85: 
   86:     CONSTRAINT CK_experiences_dates
   87:         CHECK (updated_at >= created_at)
   88: );
   89: 
   90: /*
   91:  * Owner-scoped listing and filtering.
   92:  */
   93: 
   94: CREATE INDEX IX_experiences_user_status_updated
   95:     ON dbo.experiences (
   96:         user_id,
   97:         record_status,
   98:         updated_at DESC
   99:     )
  100:     INCLUDE (
  101:         position_title,
  102:         organization_name,
  103:         category
  104:     );
  105: 
  106: /*
  107:  * Owner-scoped organization lookup.
  108:  */
  109: 
  110: CREATE INDEX IX_experiences_user_organization
  111:     ON dbo.experiences (
  112:         user_id,
  113:         organization_name
  114:     )
  115:     INCLUDE (

FILE: backend\src\database\runMigrations.js
==========================================================================================
Total lines: 316
SHA256: 7F73BACD5AA28964CE5EB23A23E019CE8B723B59D8F7BB531A24CED00ACFCADB
    1: import "dotenv/config";
    2: 
    3: import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
    4: import { readdir, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
    5: import path from "node:path";
    6: import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
    7: 
    8: import { closeDatabase, getDatabasePool, sql } from "../config/database.js";
    9: 
   10: import { getEnvironment, isDatabaseConfigured } from "../config/environment.js";
   11: 
   12: /*
   13:  * =========================================
   14:  * Migration Directory
   15:  * =========================================
   16:  */
   17: 
   18: const currentDirectory = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
   19: 
   20: const migrationsDirectory = path.join(currentDirectory, "migrations");
   21: 
   22: /*
   23:  * =========================================
   24:  * Known Migration Checksum Exception
   25:  * =========================================
   26:  *
   27:  * Migration 005 was applied to Azure SQL before its
   28:  * current file version was committed.
   29:  *
   30:  * The expected schema has been inspected in Azure.
   31:  * This exception is restricted to the two known hashes.
   32:  *
   33:  * Remove only after the migration history has been
   34:  * properly reconciled.
   35:  */
   36: 
   37: const knownChecksumExceptions = new Map([
   38:   [
   39:     "005_profile_details.sql",
   40:     {
   41:       recordedChecksum:
   42:         "f436e5fcefd0bcbd0203f816bbaa30595b1fa59811ab58d84cf781b2687ec049",
   43: 
   44:       currentChecksum:
   45:         "3817285481562c3ecb195e3f31736429c82f0879c48dac38244b77416aee365b",
   46:     },
   47:   ],
   48: ]);
   49: 
   50: const migrationFilePattern = /^\d{3,}_[a-z0-9_]+\.sql$/;
   51: 
   52: /*
   53:  * =========================================
   54:  * Migration Checksum
   55:  * =========================================
   56:  */
   57: 
   58: function createChecksum(contents) {
   59:   return createHash("sha256").update(contents, "utf8").digest("hex");
   60: }
   61: 
   62: /*
   63:  * =========================================
   64:  * Load Migration Files
   65:  * =========================================
   66:  */
   67: 
   68: async function loadMigrationFiles() {
   69:   const entries = await readdir(migrationsDirectory, {
   70:     withFileTypes: true,
   71:   });
   72: 
   73:   const fileNames = entries
   74:     .filter((entry) => entry.isFile() && migrationFilePattern.test(entry.name))
   75:     .map((entry) => entry.name)
   76:     .sort((first, second) => first.localeCompare(second, "en"));
   77: 
   78:   return Promise.all(
   79:     fileNames.map(async (fileName) => {
   80:       const filePath = path.join(migrationsDirectory, fileName);
   81: 
   82:       const contents = await readFile(filePath, "utf8");
   83: 
   84:       if (!contents.trim()) {
   85:         throw new Error(`Migration file is empty: ${fileName}`);
   86:       }
   87: 
   88:       /*
   89:        * GO is understood by SSMS, but it is not
   90:        * a SQL Server statement and cannot be sent
   91:        * through the Node MSSQL driver.
   92:        */
   93: 
   94:       if (/^\s*GO\s*;?\s*$/im.test(contents)) {
   95:         throw new Error(
   96:           `Migration ${fileName} contains GO. Remove GO because it is a client-side batch separator.`,
   97:         );
   98:       }
   99: 
  100:       return {
  101:         id: fileName,
  102: 
  103:         contents,
  104: 
  105:         checksum: createChecksum(contents),
  106:       };
  107:     }),
  108:   );
  109: }
  110: 
  111: /*
  112:  * =========================================
  113:  * Migration Tracking Table
  114:  * =========================================
  115:  */
  116: 
  117: async function ensureMigrationTable(pool) {
  118:   await pool.request().batch(`
  119:     IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.schema_migrations', N'U') IS NULL
  120:     BEGIN
  121:       CREATE TABLE dbo.schema_migrations
  122:       (
  123:         migration_id NVARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  124: 
  125:         checksum CHAR(64) NOT NULL,
  126: 
  127:         applied_at DATETIME2(3) NOT NULL
  128:           CONSTRAINT DF_schema_migrations_applied_at
  129:           DEFAULT SYSUTCDATETIME(),
  130: 
  131:         CONSTRAINT PK_schema_migrations
  132:           PRIMARY KEY CLUSTERED (migration_id)
  133:       );
  134:     END;
  135:   `);
  136: }
  137: 
  138: /*
  139:  * =========================================
  140:  * Migration Lock
  141:  * =========================================
  142:  */
  143: 
  144: async function acquireMigrationLock(request) {
  145:   const result = await request.query(`
  146:     DECLARE @lock_result INT;
  147: 
  148:     EXEC @lock_result = sys.sp_getapplock
  149:       @Resource = N'portfolio-platform-schema-migrations',
  150:       @LockMode = N'Exclusive',
  151:       @LockOwner = N'Transaction',
  152:       @LockTimeout = 60000;
  153: 
  154:     SELECT @lock_result AS lock_result;
  155:   `);
  156: 
  157:   const lockResult = Number(result.recordset[0]?.lock_result);
  158: 
  159:   if (lockResult < 0) {
  160:     throw new Error(
  161:       `Unable to acquire the database migration lock. Result: ${lockResult}`,
  162:     );
  163:   }
  164: }
  165: 
  166: /*
  167:  * =========================================
  168:  * Applied Migration Lookup
  169:  * =========================================
  170:  */
  171: 
  172: async function getAppliedMigrations(request) {
  173:   const result = await request.query(`
  174:     SELECT
  175:       migration_id,
  176:       checksum,
  177:       applied_at
  178:     FROM dbo.schema_migrations
  179:     ORDER BY migration_id;
  180:   `);
  181: 
  182:   return new Map(
  183:     result.recordset.map((migration) => [migration.migration_id, migration]),
  184:   );
  185: }
  186: 
  187: /*
  188:  * =========================================
  189:  * Run Migrations
  190:  * =========================================
  191:  */
  192: 
  193: async function runMigrations() {
  194:   const environment = getEnvironment();
  195: 
  196:   if (!isDatabaseConfigured(environment)) {
  197:     throw new Error("Database configuration is required to run migrations.");
  198:   }
  199: 
  200:   const migrations = await loadMigrationFiles();
  201: 
  202:   if (migrations.length === 0) {
  203:     console.log("No database migration files were found.");
  204: 
  205:     return;
  206:   }
  207: 
  208:   const pool = await getDatabasePool();
  209: 
  210:   await ensureMigrationTable(pool);
  211: 
  212:   const transaction = new sql.Transaction(pool);
  213: 
  214:   await transaction.begin(sql.ISOLATION_LEVEL.SERIALIZABLE);
  215: 
  216:   try {
  217:     const request = new sql.Request(transaction);
  218: 
  219:     await acquireMigrationLock(request);
  220: 
  221:     const appliedMigrations = await getAppliedMigrations(request);
  222: 
  223:     let appliedCount = 0;
  224: 
  225:     for (const migration of migrations) {
  226:       const existingMigration = appliedMigrations.get(migration.id);
  227: 
  228:       if (existingMigration) {
  229:         if (existingMigration.checksum !== migration.checksum) {
  230:           const exception = knownChecksumExceptions.get(migration.id);
  231: 
  232:           const isKnownMismatch =
  233:             exception !== undefined &&
  234:             existingMigration.checksum === exception.recordedChecksum &&
  235:             migration.checksum === exception.currentChecksum;
  236: 
  237:           if (!isKnownMismatch) {
  238:             throw new Error(
  239:               `Migration ${migration.id} was modified after it was applied. Create a new migration instead.`,
  240:             );
  241:           }
  242: 
  243:           console.warn(
  244:             `WARNING: Accepted documented checksum mismatch for ${migration.id}. ` +
  245:               "The migration was previously applied; its SQL will not be executed again.",
  246:           );
  247:         }
  248: 
  249:         console.log(`Already applied: ${migration.id}`);
  250: 
  251:         continue;
  252:       }
  253: 
  254:       console.log(`Applying migration: ${migration.id}`);
  255: 
  256:       await new sql.Request(transaction).batch(migration.contents);
  257: 
  258:       await new sql.Request(transaction)
  259:         .input("migrationId", sql.NVarChar(255), migration.id)
  260:         .input("checksum", sql.Char(64), migration.checksum).query(`
  261:           INSERT INTO dbo.schema_migrations
  262:           (
  263:             migration_id,
  264:             checksum
  265:           )
  266:           VALUES
  267:           (
  268:             @migrationId,
  269:             @checksum
  270:           );
  271:         `);
  272: 
  273:       appliedCount += 1;
  274: 
  275:       console.log(`Applied migration: ${migration.id}`);
  276:     }
  277: 
  278:     await transaction.commit();
  279: 
  280:     console.log(
  281:       appliedCount === 0
  282:         ? "Database schema is already up to date."
  283:         : `Database migration completed. Applied ${appliedCount} migration(s).`,
  284:     );
  285:   } catch (error) {
  286:     try {
  287:       await transaction.rollback();
  288:     } catch (rollbackError) {
  289:       console.error("Database migration rollback failed:", rollbackError);
  290:     }
  291: 
  292:     throw error;
  293:   }
  294: }
  295: 
  296: /*
  297:  * =========================================
  298:  * Execute Migration Process
  299:  * =========================================
  300:  */
  301: 
  302: runMigrations()
  303:   .catch((error) => {
  304:     console.error("Database migration failed:", error);
  305: 
  306:     process.exitCode = 1;
  307:   })
  308:   .finally(async () => {
  309:     try {
  310:       await closeDatabase();
  311:     } catch (error) {
  312:       console.error("Unable to close the database connection:", error);
  313: 
  314:       process.exitCode = 1;
  315:     }
  316:   });
